## Amendment 9 (2026-11-13): open simulation books
Written 2026-11-13T18:1xZ (`date -u` 18:12:55Z) for manager23, at main d1e752be; revised 2026-11-13T18:24Z (`date -u` 18:24:00Z) on the QP and reviewer verdicts on PR #1360 (QP replacement texts for §3, §4 and line (l) applied verbatim), rebased on main 3d22ad1c. Nothing here says FLOOR or any variant makes money; **no book has cleared the promotion gate**. No promotion-gate threshold changes.

**Decider and ground.** manager23, on the owner's direction of 2026-11-13 (a chat message before 17:49:23Z, the stamp of the manager's record of it). The manager's record of the owner's chat message (`memory/owner-open-sims-1113.md`, shortened there) has these two sentences:
> "We need to start running simulations and forward tests as if they're trading live at certain points in which we think would start to generate more profit and see how they are doing in real time. … That way if we see FLOOR with more money in each trade or more entries doing well, we can start to get what's live doing the same thing."

Sources: `/data/mal/hunt-1008/ops/reports/2026-11-13/open-sim-board-design.md` §1, §3, §5; `/data/mal/hunt-1008/ops/QP-RULING-1113-open-sims.md` Q4; the QP verdicts on PR #1357 (`ops/reports/2026-11-13/pr1357-qp.md`).

**1. The class.** An open simulation book is a paper replay of a named rule and configuration on the tip tape as it arrives, from a recorded start instant, with every parameter fixed at that instant. Its trades and results are shown to the owner as they happen and are not sealed. It is not a registration, not a DEC-031 arena book and not a gate read. No open-simulation trade counts toward the promotion gate or a §6 read, is pooled with real fills, or becomes part of a later read's data. A change of parameter, rule code or simulator version is a new book with a new start instant; the old book is stopped or left running, never edited. Entries before the start instant are labelled in-sample, also when they close after it.

**2. Honesty rules (QP Q4; each is kept because breaking it puts money behind a number that will not repeat).**
1. Fixed parameters per book; a tweak is a new book with a new start instant. Otherwise the record shows money the current settings never made.
2. Every number is labelled in-sample or forward. In-sample numbers overstate; mixed into a forward total they make a losing configuration look positive.
3. The board says how many book rows are watched, and the best one is treated as shrunk. With 10 zero-edge configurations the best still shows a profit by chance (DEC-031 Amendment 3 item 3).
4. Per-day totals and the total after dropping the top 3 trades are always shown. One lucky day or token can carry the whole sum (EXP-017).
5. Before a sim steers live, one book at the exact live configuration is compared with live fills trade by trade (item 3 below). FLOOR's probe exits filled −326 to −780 bps worse than modelled.
6. Honest costs at the simulated size: real fees per send, rent, priority, impact at that stake, and both fail models until live fills measure them. Leaving them out invents profit.
7. A sim that overlaps a registered read is declared in writing before its first output (§7). An undeclared peek voids the read.

**Not required for open sims** (they protect claims an open sim does not make): read dates, seals on the sim's own results, P0 floors, N accounting, and outcome-blind read tools. They stay unchanged for registered reads and the gate.

**3. When a sim may steer the live bot.** Only as one step inside this DEC's limits, with the owner's word where §2 or an amendment requires it, and only while the control book (the exact live configuration: design §1 book A) reconciles with live fills inside the bands below. On any band failure every book is marked "SIM OFF: not decision-grade", no live step is taken from the board, and the owner is told the same day; a simulator fix is a new simulator version and every book reruns from its own start under it.

Initial constants (QP may adjust them on the reconciliation tool's PR):
- **H1** live entries with no control-book entry = 0.
- **H2** `unexplained` sim-only entries = 0 per day.
- **H3** exit reason equal on every matched trade, except coded cases (host down at the take-profit, so the executor's 24 h clock sold).
- **S1** (rolling 20 matched, needs ≥ 10) median entry slip in [−1.5, +3.0] pp; at most 2 trades beyond ±8 pp.
- **S2** median (live − sim) net within ±3 pp of stake; |Σlive − Σsim| ≤ 2% of Σstake.
- **S3** live fees per trade ≤ model + 0.5 pp of stake; unreclaimed rent 0.
- **S4** live landing p50 ≤ 1.9 s and p90 ≤ 3.0 s.

**A book may suggest a live step only with this disclosure, and the manager takes such a step on its own authority only when the floor holds.** Disclosure, in every proposal: the book id and start instant; forward n closed and n complete forward days; days positive / days; forward net SOL and the total after dropping the top 3 trades, under both the flat 15% and the pressure fail legs; the same figures for the control book on the same forward days, and the paired difference; the live-minus-sim bias of the control book, (Σlive − Σsim) / Σstake, from the reconciliation; and the sentence "best of <n_books_watched> book rows; treat as shrunk". Floor: forward total after dropping the top 3 trades > 0 under both fail legs, and forward net minus the control book's net on the same days > 0 under both legs. Below the floor, the step needs the owner's explicit word for that step, and the proposal says "below the QP floor". The floor and the disclosure never permit a stake step (Amendment 6 gate, §2).

Design §5 item 4's minimum before a move (at least 7 complete forward days and 30 forward closed trades, beating A on ex-top-3 and on the per-day majority) is not adopted: QP ruled the floor above the smallest minimum required, with no day count and no waiting beyond the data itself (QP verdict on PR #1360, B2).

Until the reconciliation exists, and while it reports INSUFFICIENT (fewer than 10 matched trades) or any band fails, the board says "NOT COMPARABLE: not decision-grade". A board number may then be shown to the owner with that label, but no live step rests on it: no stake step ever rests on a board number (stake steps follow the Amendment 6 gate and §2), and a rule or parameter step at an unchanged or lower stake is taken only on the owner's explicit word for that step, quoted in the record. A live step taken on a board proposal is reverted if the control book later fails a band, unless the owner says otherwise. **The Amendment 6 gate (20 closed trades at 0.10 SOL before the 0.25 SOL stake, DEC-035 Amendment 6) is not changed by this amendment.**

**4. Books at start (v0, PR #1357, merged d1e752be).** From the daily job `scripts/research/floor_forward_ab.sh` (replay from 2026-11-02T01Z), forward-from 2026-11-12 (entries on or after 2026-11-12T00:00Z; earlier entries are in-sample, including those that close later). The forward-from was declared in code at 2026-11-12T21:43:03Z (commit 0982c931, PR #1324), after the start instant but before any output on 2026-11-12 data, and the rule and parameters were fixed on 2026-11-03..11-11 data; the board labels these books "v0 replay, forward-from 2026-11-12 declared 2026-11-12T21:43Z". The 0.5 and 1.0 rungs are priced by code merged 2026-11-13 (d1e752be) over the same decisions:
- **D0**: `l1-floor-v2` as live, without the lane gap guard;
- **C0**: vault1.0 (`Q_STAR=1.0e9`), without the gap guard; picked from a variant search on 2026-11-03..11-11.

Each at 0.10, 0.25, 0.5 and 1.0 SOL, labelled as QP required on #1357: 0.10 "model; live fills exist, not reconciled (recon NOT COMPARABLE)"; 0.25 "model; live check pending"; 0.5 and 1.0 "model only, extrapolated; not for steering". Neither v0 book is the control book. A day is complete only when the tape runs at least 90 min past its end.

**Later books** (control A under the lane guard, and C, D1, E1, E2 from design §1; the follow-mode driver) start under this amendment when their tool PRs merge. Each is recorded by a MiScusi notebook entry with its manifest sha256 and start instant. No further amendment is needed per book.

**5. SEALS-BRIEF lines.** The brief is a host file the manager edits. Each line below is in force when its tool's PR merges and the manager adds the line naming the merged commit; (j)'s PR is #1357 (d1e752be).
- **(j)**, verbatim from QP's verdict on #1357:
  > (j) OPEN SIM BOARD v0 (PR #1357): `floor_forward_ab.py board` reads only the two pnl json files of one floor_forward_ab.sh work dir under /data2/mal/scratch/floor-forward-ab and writes board.json and board.md there; the manager may copy exactly those two files (no other file of the work dir) to ~/data/open-sim/ on research-0 for the Console. They carry no pool id, mint, signature, price or reserve. Paper only; an open simulation, not a gate read.
- **(k)**, draft:
  > (k) OPEN SIM FOLLOW DRIVER (PR #[n], commit [sha]): scripts/research/osb_follow.py reads ONLY closed hv7 hours that the arena tip receiver's verification log lists, hard-linked under /data2/mal/scratch/floor-open-sim (its keeper dir and per-book dirs); it writes only under /data2/mal/scratch/floor-open-sim and to ~/data/open-sim on research-0. Files under ~/data/open-sim carry no pool id, mint, signature, price or reserve. Paper only; an open simulation, not a gate read.
- **(l)**, draft:
  > (l) OPEN SIM LIVE EXTRACT AND RECONCILIATION (PR #[n], commit [sha]): scripts/ops/osb_live_extract.py runs on mal-fast-0 only as a manager job with sudo -n; it reads the floor-lane-1 decision rows (entry, exit, entry_refused_gap, entry_gap_span_ignored, lane_restart_shadow(_failed), start), the bridge log rows (buy_sent, buy_landed, sell_sent, sell_landed, refusals with reason, gap and gap_hold), the lane ledger cash rows and the fills log fields spend_lamports, tokens_received and price_vs_quote_bps; it writes one 0600 extract keyed by order id and copies it to research-0 (0640, readable only by the tool's user); stdout is counts and sha256 only. scripts/ops/osb_recon.py on research-0 is the only reader of the extract; no agent opens, lists beyond its name, copies or parses it. It may print, per order id, only: order id; sim trade id; entry and exit UTC to the second; stake SOL; class; reason code; entry slip pp; exit slip pp; landing latency ms (landed minus decision, an integer, never an absolute instant); live and sim exit reason; live and sim net SOL; live fees in pp of stake (sends, priority, pool fees, unreclaimed rent) and the model's fees in pp of stake. Window aggregates may add counts, medians and p90s of those fields. Pool, mint, signature, wallet, slot, absolute price and reserves never leave the extract and the tool. If a DEC-035 §6 registration covers lane trades in the window, the output is declared under §7 before its first print.

**6. Ledger.** One HOLDOUT_LEDGER changelog line (this PR) records the hours as open FLOOR information.

**Outside this amendment.** r4-b (DEC-031 Amendment 31, PR #1358).

**Unchanged.** §2 (every limit, the STOP files, keys), §3–§7, the Amendment 6 gate and its count, Amendments 1–8, and the promotion gate. Nothing here says FLOOR or any variant makes money; no book has cleared the promotion gate.

## Amendment 9, Erratum 1 (2026-11-14): pricing-only twin books (gap-in-hold trades)
Written 2026-11-14 for manager23, at main 3c3d1920. Records only; changes no threshold, window, pin or clock. Sources: the QP ruling `/data/mal/hunt-1008/ops/QP-RULING-1114-board-gap-in-hold.md` (sha256 prefix f6459f42) and the QP verdict on PR #1380 (`/data/mal/hunt-1008/ops/reports/2026-11-14/pr1380-qp.md`, sha256 prefix 10bd5556).

**Text (QP, verbatim).**
> Erratum (2026-11-14, QP ruling board gap-in-hold, PR #1380): a pricing-only twin of an open simulation book (same rule, parameters, host, stakes and send; only `gap_exclusion` "hold" differs; manifest key `twin_of`) keeps its strict book's start instant if, at every board build, its decisions.md5 equals the strict book's (and it passed the first-run checks recorded in its notebook entry); while they differ it is shown as a separate book with "twin decisions differ: not a pricing-only twin". A twin's figures are compared only with the control book's twin, never with a strict book. Twin trades are open-simulation trades under §1: no gate read, §6 read or registration uses them.

**Why.** The strict board drops any trade whose 24 h hold touches a feed gap (DEC-031 §1.4, kept unchanged for arena books and gate reads). The dropped November trades were net negative (Amendment 6 Erratum 3: 29 positions, −0.6951137523 SOL at 0.25 flat), so the strict set can overstate. The live bot holds such trades, and its take-profit watch is blind during a gap when its own feed has the same gap; the twin prices them that way, so it is closer to what live holds (the recon's `tp_in_sim_gap` class shows where the live feed saw prints the twin did not).

**Mechanism.** A twin's manifest has `gap_exclusion: "hold"` and `twin_of` (the strict book's id). Tool PRs: #1380 (merge commit 3c3d1920ab7af3a8b0b7c2070627aec3e1884e62: driver, decisions md5, board pair view), #1379 (8f5c5426771a9724f3883335c4991851e3f20965: recon against the twin), #1378 (8c7061e767544c82884f1e05bee0e1de6ae8e809: recon). Twin rows add only `gap_in_hold`, `blind_s`, `gap_count`, `gap_holes` (times only) and, when greater than 0, `slot_only_gaps`. The board shows the two sets side by side, gap-in-hold trades first, and never sums them.

**First-run conditions (QP on #1380).** Before any twin output reaches the board:
1. C1: a new 48 h osb_proof PASS at the merge commit M of #1380 (SEALS (k) procedure).
2. C2: on-path identity on real data. For each strict manifest that gets a twin, run an "on" copy at M (a new scratch book id and out_root, never the live book) over the 48 h proof window of job #2632. Every hour's ledger_md5 and decision_md5 must equal the strict book's stored md5-by-hour.jsonl values.
3. C3: run each twin at M over the same window. Its decisions md5 (osb_follow.book_decisions_md5) must equal its strict book's at the same last hour, and its per-hour decision_md5 must be equal for every hour.
4. C4: the md5 of the twin's trade rows without `gap_in_hold` equals the md5 of the strict book's trade rows for the same position_ids (canonical lines sorted by position_id and stake, `written_ms` removed).
5. C5: per stake, strict `feed_gap` = twin `feed_gap_entry` + `feed_gap_exit` + twin trade rows flagged `gap_in_hold` (including slot-only and exit_invalid ones).
6. C7: this erratum and the SEALS (k)/(m)/(o) additions are in place before the first twin output. The C2–C5 md5s and counts are recorded in the twins' MiScusi notebook entries.

(C6, blockers 1–2 fixed at a new head with QP OK, was met at 9c1e930a.)

**Limits.** The strict books stay at commit 457795f4. A strict book moves to a later commit only as a new book, or after a full-history md5 check (a restart at the new commit re-derives every stored hour and finds every stored decision_md5 and ledger_md5 equal) under a further erratum. Neither set is decision-grade while the reconciliation says NOT COMPARABLE. No stake, budget or live step rests on either (Amendment 6). Paper only. No book has cleared the promotion gate.

## Amendment 9, Erratum 2 (2026-11-14): pricing-only latency twins (entry latency)
In force from its merge; no latency twin runs before L1 to L7 below are met. Written 2026-11-14T11:40Z (`date -u`) for manager23. Records only; changes no threshold, window, pin or clock. Source: the QP ruling `/data/mal/hunt-1008/ops/QP-RULING-1114-osb-latency-twin.md` (sha256 prefix cd2bfde2), sections 3 to 7. The code is the PR `claude/osb-latency-twin` (the code PR, number to be filled by the manager at merge; not merged at writing).

**Text (QP, verbatim).**
> Erratum 2 (2026-11-14, QP ruling osb latency twin): a pricing-only latency twin of an open simulation book (same rule, parameters, host, stakes, send and gap_exclusion "on"; only the engine's entry latency `pricing_lat_entry_s` differs, from the declared set 3.010, 4.085, 5.590 s of total chain time from decision to landing; manifest key `twin_of`) keeps its strict book's start instant if, at every board build, its decisions.md5 equals the strict book's and it passed the first-run checks recorded in its notebook entry; while they differ it is shown as a separate book with "twin decisions differ: not a pricing-only twin". An exit decided before the twin's entry lands is excluded as `exit_before_entry_landing`, counted and shown, never priced. A latency twin's figures are compared only with the control book's latency twin of the same value, never with a strict book or a twin of another value. The latency values were fixed from live timings (job #2702) before any twin output; no other value is used without a further erratum. Latency-twin trades are open-simulation trades under §1: no gate read, §6 read or registration uses them. No stake, limit or live step rests on them.

**What it changes.** It adds a second kind of pricing-only twin beside Erratum 1's gap twin. Latency does not change FLOOR decisions in this engine (QP ruling, verdict on questions 1 and 2: entries and exits are host decisions on tape rows; the take-profit gate and mark use the rule's constants LAT_DECIDING = 1.9 and FEED_D = 7, not the engine's latency), so the twin changes only the engine's entry k for the deciding leg, and its decisions.md5 is expected equal to the strict book's.

**Values, declared before any output (fixed; cannot be retuned).** Section 3 of the ruling:
- **LT-a "post-Am.10 expectation": `pricing_lat_entry_s` = 3.010** (14 slots x 0.215 s).
- **LT-b "live now": `pricing_lat_entry_s` = 4.085** (19 slots x 0.215 s, the median decision-to-landed of the 12 live FLOOR buys in job #2702).
- **LT-c "slowest live": `pricing_lat_entry_s` = 5.590** (26 slots x 0.215 s, the slowest of the same 12).
- Entry only; the exit is unchanged in all three. One twin per value for the control (D-type) book; a twin per value for a variant only if that variant already has a book.
- Derivation (ruling, section 3): job #2702 (median and maximum decision-to-landed slots of the 12 live buys, timings only); 0.215 s/slot from the A3 record 213 to 215 ms/slot.
- **Declaration.** Before the first twin output, the manager writes a MiScusi notebook entry (kind decision) holding the three values, their derivation and the manifests' sha256, and the sentence: "These latency values were fixed from live timings before any twin output was seen; no other value is used without a new Am.9 erratum; a later measured value (e.g. after Am.10 installs) is a new twin with a new start instant beside these, not a change to them."

**Mechanism (ruling, section 2; derived from code reading, not measured).** `pricing_lat_entry_s` is the total chain time from decision to landing, in the same frame as the live "decision slot to landed slot" figure. For the deciding leg only (ledger tag "1p9"): `k_twin = max(0, ceil(pricing_lat_entry_s / sps - SLOT_TOL) - d)`, d = feed_delay_slots, sps the position's own measured sps_entry. The 1.3 leg and the exit lag are unchanged. Every trade and excluded row of a latency twin carries `pricing_lat_entry_s` and `k_entry_slots` in its `stack` block. With the option absent the engine is byte-identical to today's.

**Rows that can differ (ruling, section 1).** (a) An exit decided before the twin's buy lands is not priced: an `excluded` row, reason `exit_before_entry_landing`, counted and shown, never dropped silently. (b) Entry state, the 15% guard, ssb and nearby (pressure leg), fees by tier and the exit with our own buy re-injected are priced as the engine does at the later landing. (c) The take-profit mark stays the rule's 1.9 s mark, so a later fill makes the realised take-profit return smaller; that is the live effect, kept. (d) Exclusion spans and `need` start from the decision slot and use x_max = sd + d + max k, so a larger k can add a `feed_gap` or `missing_v` exclusion or delay resolution of a position whose exit falls within a few seconds of its entry; counted by reason. decisions.md5 is unaffected, because the exit decision ms is written on both excluded and trade rows; at most a transient difference at the board hour for such positions, which the board shows as "pending". (e) Nothing else: if any other row type differs, the twin refuses to start (exit 2) until QP rules.

**First-run conditions (ruling, section 4; the strict books stay at 457795f4).**
- **L1.** The tool PR merged at commit ML; a 48 h osb_proof PASS at ML.
- **L2.** Off-path identity: with `pricing_lat_entry_s` absent, an "on" copy of each strict manifest that gets a twin, run at ML over job #2632's 48 h window, has every hour's ledger_md5 and decision_md5 equal to the strict book's stored values.
- **L3.** Each latency twin at ML, over the same window: decisions md5 (osb_follow.book_decisions_md5) equal to its strict book's at the same last hour, and per-hour decision_md5 equal for every hour.
- **L4.** Leg identity: for positions priced in both books, the md5 of (position_id, stake, legs.total.flat_1p3, legs.total.press_1p3, calc."1p3") is equal between twin and strict, because the 1.3 leg is untouched.
- **L5.** Conservation per stake: twin priced + twin excluded (by reason, incl. `exit_before_entry_landing`) = strict priced + strict excluded, over the same position_ids.
- **L6.** QP OK on the tool PR at its exact head, with a fixture test: a twin whose `pricing_lat_entry_s` gives k_twin = 9 at sps 0.215 reproduces the strict rows byte for byte, apart from the two new stack fields.
- **L7.** Am.9 Erratum 2 merged, and SEALS (k)/(m) lines amended to name the latency twins' manifest keys and row fields, before the first output.
- Record the L2 to L5 md5s and counts in each twin's notebook entry.

**Board label and recon use (ruling, section 5).** Driver: manifest keys `pricing_lat_entry_s` (a number from the declared set, nothing else) and `twin_of`; `gap_exclusion` must be "on" (one dimension per twin). Board: pair view beside its own strict book, the latency twin first, never summed; compared only with the control book's latency twin of the SAME `pricing_lat_entry_s`, otherwise n/a (the control's own latency twin gets no comparison); `exit_before_entry_landing` counted and shown.
- Label (no "price" or "slot" word, which the board's guard refuses): "latency twin: entry lands <L> s after the decision (chain time; live median 4.085 s on 12 buys, job #2702); exits and decisions as the strict book; take-profit mark unchanged, as live".
- Line: "exit before entry landed (<twin>): n (not counted as a trade)".
- Recon: it may show S1 (entry slip) and S2 (net) against LT-b as a labelled diagnostic column. The bands (H1 to H3, S1 to S4) and the recon status stay computed against the control book (strict model) until a QP ruling on a recon PR moves the comparison. S4 stays as written (line 715; `osb_recon.py` 1,900 / 3,000 ms and k 5 / 8 at 0.4 s steps). It does not use the strict model's landing (about 3.44 s after the decision, Amendment 10 Erratum 1). Recalibrating it is a QP ruling on a recon PR (Amendment 10 item 7).

**What must not be concluded (ruling, section 6).**
- No stake, limit, budget or live step rests on a latency twin (Am.6, Am.9 §3). Neither set is decision-grade while the recon says NOT COMPARABLE.
- The latency values are not a choice among results. No value may be picked, dropped or replaced after seeing its numbers.
- Every twin row adds to n_books_watched and the best-of-N sentence.
- A latency twin measures the cost of slower fills on the same decisions. It is not evidence that any book makes money, and not evidence about Am.10's live effect until live fills after Am.10 are reconciled.

**What it does not change.** The strict books (changing their latency or d is a new simulator version, so new books, Am.9 §1; shown first in twins), Erratum 1's gap twins, any limit, threshold, window, pin or clock, and the promotion gate. No book has cleared the promotion gate.

## Amendment 9, Erratum 3 (2026-11-14): C2r / L2r replace C2 and L2 (ledger md5 is not comparable across commits)
In force from its merge. Written 2026-11-14T14:30Z (`date -u`) for manager23, at main ef5545ad. Records only; changes no threshold, window, pin or clock. Source: the QP ruling addendum `/data/mal/hunt-1008/ops/QP-RULING-1114-board-gap-in-hold-ADD1.md` (sha256 prefix 9736edc9). As with Amendment 7 Erratum 1 and Amendment 9 Erratum 2, Erratum 1 and Erratum 2 are not edited in place; this block follows them and controls where they differ. It replaces C2 of Erratum 1 and L2 of Erratum 2, and the "Limits" sentence of Erratum 1 quoted below. Nothing else in Amendment 9 or its errata changes.

**Finding.** Condition C2 of Erratum 1 and L2 of Erratum 2 compare a per-hour `ledger_md5` across two commits. `ArenaEngine.buy` writes `code_head` (with `code_dirty`) into every `open` row (`tools/arena_paper.py` lines 1131 to 1133 at main ef5545ad: `head, dirty = self.code_state()` and the `open` row append carrying `"code_head": head, "code_dirty": dirty`), so the equality cannot hold between two commits, by construction. It was first seen on the first real run: the twin proof job #2669 PASSed, and the twin check job #2729 had C3, C4 and C5 PASS and C2 FAIL on 36 of 62 hours, from the first entry hour 2026-11-12T14, with the decision md5 equal in every hour (timings and md5 verdicts as given by the manager; not re-run by the drafter). The per-hour `ledger_md5` is `osb_follow.tree_md5`, which already drops `engine_id` and `start` rows (QP code reading); what still differs is the code identity.

**Replacement condition (QP, verbatim; C2r for gap twins, L2r for latency twins, same text).**
> C2r / L2r: over the identical window, (i) every hour's decision_md5 of the on-copy run equals the strict run's, and (ii) the md5 of the ledger projection of the on-copy run's final ledger equals the strict run's. The projection is osb_follow.ledger_rows (start rows dropped, as stated) with `engine_id`, `code_head` and `written_ms` removed, in ledger file order. `code_dirty` stays in and must be false in both runs. The tool also prints the multiset md5 of the same projection, the row counts by kind (open, trade, excluded and any other kind) and the set of keys that differ between paired rows (must be empty).

- Use the ordered projection (stronger than the multiset). If the ordered md5 differs but the multiset md5 is equal, report it as FAIL with "order only". QP rules on that case if it ever happens.
- Every row kind except `start` is compared, including `late_print` or any other kind present, not only open/trade/excluded.
- **Code-identity fields: complete list.** The engine has no list. These are all of them, found by the writers of process or code state in tools/arena_paper.py:
  - `engine_id` (uuid at init, :854);
  - `code_head` and `code_dirty` (`code_state()`, on `open` rows :1131-1133 and on `start` rows :1026-1028);
  - `pid`, `decision_ms` and `trade_id` of `start` rows (:1026-1028);
  - `written_ms` (`now_fn`, every row; the engine's own `identity()` already excludes it).

  To re-verify on any later commit, grep tools/arena_paper.py and scripts/research/floor_gap_range_engine.py for `engine_id`, `code_state(`, `getpid` and `now_fn(` at row writers. The "differing keys" print in C2r is the runtime catch-all.
- **L2r** (latency twins), the ruling's text: "L2r: the C2r text above with "latency" for "gap": on-copy at ML vs the strict run over the identical window; per-hour decision_md5 equal; ledger projection (without engine_id, code_head, written_ms; start rows dropped) equal; code_dirty false in both."
- The tool prints the old C2 and L2 as "not comparable across commits", never PASS or FAIL.

**Limits sentence replaced.** Erratum 1, "Limits" (line 765 of this file before this erratum), says: "A strict book moves to a later commit only as a new book, or after a full-history md5 check (a restart at the new commit re-derives every stored hour and finds every stored decision_md5 and ledger_md5 equal) under a further erratum." It is replaced by (QP, verbatim):
> a strict book may move to a later commit and keep its id and start instant only if a restart at that commit re-derives every stored hour with every stored decision_md5 equal and its final ledger projection (ledger_rows without engine_id, code_head and written_ms) equal to the book's current one; any mismatch makes the move a new book.

The other sentences of that paragraph (strict books stay at commit 457795f4; neither set is decision-grade while the reconciliation says NOT COMPARABLE; no stake, budget or live step rests on either; paper only) stand.

**Order of work (QP; the DEC text controls, an addendum cannot replace it).**
- Gap twin A-gh may START now as a paper run in scratch. Erratum 1's conditions apply "Before any twin output reaches the board". Its output must not reach the board, and it may not claim the strict book's start instant, until three things are done:
  - (a) this erratum (Am.9 Erratum 3) is merged, replacing C2 in Erratum 1 and L2 in Erratum 2 with the C2r/L2r text above, and correcting Erratum 1's "Limits" sentence;
  - (b) the osb_twin_check change (conditions C2r/L2r; old C2/L2 reported as "not comparable across commits", never PASS or FAIL) is merged with QP OK (the osb_twin_check change, PR number filled by the manager at merge);
  - (c) C2r has run and PASSed on the same three runs (#2662 M0/L1, #2669 M0/L1, #2669 M1/L1), with the PASS recorded in A-gh's notebook entry.
- Latency twins: Erratum 2 says "no latency twin runs before L1 to L7", so none runs before (a), (b) and an L2r PASS.

**Thin row evidence (QP; C4: 1 position / 4 rows; C5: 1 gap position per stake).**
- These are exact identity checks of deterministic code, not estimates. A PASS on few rows is valid but covers few code paths.
- No longer proof window is owed before board output. Instead, while the pair runs, the manager re-runs C3/C4/C5 on the live strict book and its twin at a common last hour once a day. This continues until both of these hold:
  - at least 20 positions are priced in both books;
  - at least 5 `gap_in_hold` rows exist, or 7 days have passed.
- Record the counts each day. Any FAIL shows the twin as a separate book at once.
- Until `feed_gap_entry`, `feed_gap_exit` and `exit_invalid_across_gap` have each occurred on real tape, the pair view adds:
  > identity checked on real tape for n positions (gap paths not yet seen on real tape: <list>; synthetic tests cover them)

**The proof tool's own PASS is unaffected (QP).** osb_proof compares legs at ONE commit (same `code_head`), using `tree_md5`, which drops `engine_id` and start rows. Its PASS on #2669 (and #2662, #2723) stands. A26a0766 vs a470b34d is the expected difference between A and A-gh: the one gap position priced. The driver's restart check (stored ledger_md5) is same-commit by its code pin, so it is also unaffected.

**What it does not change.** Every other condition of Erratum 1 (C1, C3, C4, C5, C7) and of Erratum 2 (L1, L3 to L7), the declared latency values, the board rules, Amendment 6 and the promotion gate. Neither set is decision-grade while the reconciliation says NOT COMPARABLE, and no stake, limit or live step rests on a twin. A twin's identity check says the pricing code agrees with the strict code on those rows; it is not evidence that any book makes money. No book has cleared the promotion gate.

## Amendment 9, Erratum 4 (2026-11-14): the board pairs a twin with its strict book on the host decision md5, at a common hour
In force from its merge. Records only; changes no threshold, window, pin or clock, and no code. Source: the QP ruling addendum `/data/mal/hunt-1008/ops/QP-RULING-1114-board-gap-in-hold-ADD2.md` (sha256 `e9cfb10833f6a24f356d081f72d1323146040662083f12edcaeadbec30262e98`). The text below is QP's, from the ruling's "Erratum 4 text". As with the earlier errata, Errata 1 to 3 are not edited in place; this block follows them and controls where they differ.

**Binding text (QP, verbatim).**
> Am.9 Erratum 4: "its decisions.md5 equals the strict book's" in Errata 1 and 2 means, at each board build: the per-hour host decision_md5 (md5-by-hour.jsonl, floor_lane_host.decision_digest) stored for the latest hour present in both books' files is equal. The pair view, and any twin figure, is shown only when both books report the same last hour; otherwise the twin is "pending" with its rows withheld, and after 6 h pending the board says so and names both hours. A twin pending more than 24 h is stopped, or the reason is recorded in its notebook entry. A differing decision_md5 shows the twin as "twin decisions differ: not a pricing-only twin". The first-run conditions C3/L3 are unchanged.

- The stored `decision_md5` is cumulative through that hour (explanatory note, not part of the quoted text).

**Why (from the ruling).**
- A ledger row is written only when the position resolves: the pool's next print, or 10,000 slots of feed (`grace_slots`), which is unbounded in a feed hole because `hw_slot` does not advance. No ledger cut, by `written_ms` or by a fixed number of hours, is comparable between two books.
- The host decision digest is written at decision time and does not depend on pricing, on resolution or on `written_ms`.
- C3 / L3 already require it equal per hour (62 of 62 equal on the gap twin, job #2729).

**Records.**
- QP ruling: `/data/mal/hunt-1008/ops/QP-RULING-1114-board-gap-in-hold-ADD2.md`, sha256 `e9cfb10833f6a24f356d081f72d1323146040662083f12edcaeadbec30262e98`.
- Code PR #1395, merge commit `8bec1d9146433c1ed4c1d403f773cc4ef569b831` (QP OK and REVIEWER APPROVE at head `bfc8a7356fa3adfd3d02c7d2d4d779a3aa5b5aa3`). The board has run from a clean worktree at that commit since 2026-11-14T19:45:51Z (job #2753). Its first build shows the pair osb-a-gh / osb-a with both books at last hour 2026-11-14T18 and equal decision_md5.
- Earlier board builds compared `decisions.md5` files. One build, at 2026-11-14T16:30:25Z, printed "twin decisions differ" for the gap twin while it was catching up. That line was false; the twin was then taken off the board until this fix. No figure or decision rested on it.
- Owed by earlier errata: Erratum 2's code PR is #1390 (merge commit `861952ee3fb3ddd936f48f9dd071ddfd150cf504`); Erratum 3's tool PR is #1393 (merge commit `6061edb72faf1a0c173f6c2f7a8a00b928cdb885`).

**What does not change.** First-run conditions C1 to C5 and L1 to L7 (C3 / L3 as stated above), the start-instant rule, and "twin and strict sets are never summed". `book_decisions_md5` / `decisions.md5` stay what the recon uses, a separate check at the twin-run level. No stake, limit or live step rests on a board, recon or twin number.

No book has cleared the promotion gate. Nothing here says any book makes money.

(6) Overlap with the r4-b open preview, the osb books and floor-lane-1: the same post-graduation PumpSwap pools may appear in several universes.

## Amendment 9 book-list record, 2026-11-16: osb-u4 and osb-u2 (U4 and U2 open simulations)

In force from its merge. Written 2026-11-16T21:18:59Z (`date -u` 21:18:59Z; re-pinned to 49dbf7ca and deciding-only per ADD2 the same evening) by a worker for manager24, at main d179c003. Records only; changes no threshold, window, pin, clock or limit.

**Amendment 9 book-list record, 2026-11-16: osb-u4 (U4 open simulation).** Under §4 ("Later books ... start under this amendment"), one more open simulation book, not in design §1, and a different kind from the FLOOR books: U4 (diamond-share accumulation: tape-derived never-sold share NS >= 0.50, NS up >= 0.05 over 60 min and top-1 share down over 60 min, graduated WSOL pools aged [1 h, 24 h), every 5 min, once per mint). Its read rule, simulator, latency set, fee and rent modes, 5-day look and disclosures are fixed in `/data/mal/hunt-1008/ops/QP-RULING-1116-u4-u2-p81-forward-specs.md` (sha256 `297657b433180e8e8dd8233ed30d1d98885e8a3575431f0d9f054b2e26fda764`) its addendum 1 `/data/mal/hunt-1008/ops/QP-RULING-1116-u4-u2-p81-forward-specs-ADD1.md` (sha256 `e5009a3c0e3ecb2716befc62ae6fc5b2aa7553fec18a2c9f7f055085f66d6665`) and its addendum 2 `/data/mal/hunt-1008/ops/QP-RULING-1116-u4-u2-p81-forward-specs-ADD2.md` (sha256 `ff5ea10c41e6e24b1c21e5b6c08edd01a7d4c06e5a395013fd8aab6e4fccac44`) before the start instant.

- **Books.** `osb-u4` (deciding: entry latency 19 slots = 4.085 s, send 505,000 lamports per send, rent stranded: 2,039,280 lamports subtracted per fill until the live ATA close is verified (V6)); twins `osb-u4-lat3010` (14 slots) and `osb-u4-lat5590` (26 slots); fee beside `osb-u4-send160k` (160,000 per send). Stakes 0.10 / 0.25 / 0.5 SOL from the same decisions (0.5 is model only, extrapolated, not for steering). **Deciding books alone start at S** (ADD2 (a)): only `osb-u4` runs at S, with the random-pick comparison bot inside the same process (GO condition (6) needs it in the deciding cell). The twins start later only by full replay from the same W start on a contiguous keep, with the manifests and commit as recorded here, and are shown only for hours whose per-hour decision md5 equals the deciding book's (`--compare`; columns end at the first mismatch; "not available" at the look if absent). The 160,000 column is computed from the deciding ledger only after the reader's fixture-equality test (a ledger with a requested sell, a refused requested sell and a placebo bot row) passes at the pinned commit; until then it is not shown and `osb-u4-send160k` is not run (its manifest stays as recorded and unused). The four processes are separate when started, and the twins and the fee column never decide. Each process also runs the ruling's random-pick comparison book (a second bot id inside the process, same pools, same exits and pricing; seed rule: U4 seed = 1 + mark epoch seconds + draw index, one pool per book entry), reported beside and part of GO condition (6). Exit: decision instant + 4 h, or the share-fall exit (NS(M) <= 0.80 x NS at entry), whichever is earlier.
- **Manifests** (`/data2/mal/scratch/u4u2-open-sim/manifests/`, mode 0444; for each: file sha256 from `sha256sum`, canonical manifest sha256 from `c1nf_forward_sim.manifest_sha256(check_manifest(json))`, out_root): `osb-u4.json`: file sha256 `c677b2b52607bb76b5bbb75d47d39af9602e58b0ec1d316095acba109670cdfa`, canonical sha256 `b8f2c524cdabcfe6bc7d1d341c9a4dc83ccdf86ca29405cff981f6ad68624961`, out_root `/data2/mal/scratch/c1nf-open-sim/osb-u4`; `osb-u4-lat3010.json`: file sha256 `414b4b26a9577b589127bb1a90debab3278a94df7215cb9f88d202458b26ef20`, canonical sha256 `702f536d4415eff930342d4dad36e4b92641afb9a9d323b9db0c2bdbfb945efa`, out_root `/data2/mal/scratch/c1nf-open-sim/osb-u4-lat3010`; `osb-u4-lat5590.json`: file sha256 `f59f8bf53fb558bfa2ed74a804fbc9343dbd18ae3fdce1919f4d3860a9ccde00`, canonical sha256 `679c73684719c8708958c6520486f5bbb9c82688c1e3b0a6f87e7a89a7db871b`, out_root `/data2/mal/scratch/c1nf-open-sim/osb-u4-lat5590`; `osb-u4-send160k.json`: file sha256 `4613f04a47a9e770d971cf8f8cee60aafa90719468d3782ed11e2de5f8ba083d`, canonical sha256 `c83087f13a838c5e4bd1818e514c063fbd59f3204f00a8348a927867c77df5ca`, out_root `/data2/mal/scratch/c1nf-open-sim/osb-u4-send160k`. They differ only in book_id, title, out_root, pricing_lat_entry_s and send_lamports. Out roots `/data2/mal/scratch/c1nf-open-sim/<book_id>` (the merged runner's path guard allows no other root; the ruling's suggested `/data2/mal/scratch/u4-open-sim/` is not usable without a new runner commit).
- **Start and warm-up.** Start instant S = 2026-11-17T12:00:00Z (`start_hour` 2026-11-17T12; the manager moves S by re-issuing the manifests and amending this record before the start). Warm-up W = [max(2026-11-03T03:00Z, S - 336 h), S) = [2026-11-03T12:00Z, S), required warm-up >= 168 h (met: 336 h); the first post-S deltas use NS / TOP1 snapshots at marks in [S - 60 min, S) only. Decisions off in code: no universe pass, ranking, breadth, fire, entry, placebo draw, price or outcome is computed for any mark before S; the ledger holds zero rows with a decision instant before S (a row that does voids the book). A book whose W is shorter than the ruling's minimum is refused by the runner and S moves later.
- **Code and pins.** `scripts/research/c1nf_forward_sim.py` with `scripts/research/u4_u2_deciders.py` at main c58f0ee4e3ec71ee6ffda414ea421f390b4b2063 (PRs #1415, #1421, #1424, #1422, #1428 and #1432, the bounded-state fix; 49dbf7ca and 1d7695c7 are never run for a book), run as a manager MiScusi job on mal-research-0 from a clean detached worktree at that commit (`/data2/mal/wt/u4u2-c58f0ee4`), after the real-layout deciding-only dry run's proof PASS there (SEALS-BRIEF line (q)), under a systemd-run --user scope whose MemoryMax is that dry run's peak RSS over the full W plus a margin; the Python version and the sha256 of `pip freeze` of `/data/mal/venv/bin/python` are recorded at the dry run and at every book start (ADD2 (b)2). A later commit is a new simulator version and a new book. Before S the dry run's field-presence counts (V2-V5, V8; counts and shares only) are recorded here by an erratum: U4 starts only if the count of PumpSwap / bonding trade rows missing `trader`, `side` or an integer `token_raw` is 0 over W; both books only if at least 99% of `pool_create` rows carry a `quote_mint` (ADD2 (c)). These books load no model, no wallet ledger, no duckdb and no pick oracle (the runner refuses those keys for them) and write no duckdb spill; any `*/duckdb-tmp/` directory under any out_root is never opened. Out roots `/data2/mal/scratch/c1nf-open-sim/<book_id>` accepted by ADD2 (b)1 (book ids never equal or prefix a C1-NF id; empty at the first start). Input: only the osb keeper's hard links under `/data2/mal/scratch/floor-open-sim/keep/`; output only under `/data2/mal/scratch/c1nf-open-sim/`. Gap handling: strict feed-gap exclusion (ADD1 A1), with the split counters in `gap_exclusions.json`. **Pin re-issue, 2026-11-17 (osb-u4):** the pin moves from 49dbf7ca86a4b5949be77aa818a5f5162634d358 to c58f0ee4e3ec71ee6ffda414ea421f390b4b2063 (PR #1432, "u4_u2_deciders: bounded state ..."). File sha256 at the new pin: `scripts/research/u4_u2_deciders.py` `7a73ef034ff8857d8256356a55913f1dab1067facb20b587d7b2ffcff5446ccb`; `scripts/research/c1nf_forward_sim.py` `bd90fb77d4a9d083ff2270dde666ded730f7786c9d1f9b3948db807fef7273ac`; clean worktree `/data2/mal/wt/u4u2-c58f0ee4`. Reason: the deciding-only dry run at 49dbf7ca (MiScusi job #2916, systemd scope MemoryMax 10G) was OOM-killed by the scope at 2026-11-17T00:11:39Z after 270 warm-up hours of osb-u4 (to 2026-11-14T17); osb-u2 never ran; no decision or figure was produced (every hour was warm-up before S). Cause: U4 runs no mark before S - 60 min, so its warm-up state was never pruned, and U2 trimmed its series only at forward marks. Rulings: QP-RULING-1117-u4u2-bounded-state (`/data/mal/hunt-1008/ops/QP-RULING-1117-u4u2-bounded-state.md`, sha256 `622c4734fdb5d6785e69510391af8c57206b06c4356315f99e6a87c877b0af91`); QP VERDICT OK and REVIEWER VERDICT APPROVE at 670afa38c3859d78038a374035a8c6396ab61d93 on PR #1432. SEALS-BRIEF (q) is re-issued at c58f0ee4 (SEALS-BRIEF sha256 prefix af77da69; out_roots dry2-osb-u4 and dry2-osb-u2 added). Dry manifests (mode 0444, `/data2/mal/scratch/u4u2-open-sim/manifests/`): `dry2-osb-u4.json` sha256 `ef2929ee6fe4821544479009f30f868db9412eb3da05ec0feb65ea74c300a798`, `dry2-osb-u2.json` sha256 `3c6f32b63bd0f76c75fce6b4c511fdea1311d9a40d4dcbcddc7f94a169b18688`; dry run 2 is MiScusi job #2923 (script `/data/mal/hunt-1008/ops/manager24/u4u2-dry-2.sh`). The book manifests `osb-u4.json` and `osb-u2.json` and their shas above are unchanged. S stays 2026-11-17T12:00:00Z; a book may start late and catch up on closed hours (paper replay). **Before any book start (ruling "Before S", same steps as #1430 / ADD2 (d)3):** (1) SEALS-BRIEF (q) names PR #1432's merge commit and the new clean worktree before any run. (2) This T1 Am.9 pin record PR is merged before S; it gives the reason and carries the disclosure below. (3) A deciding-only dry run at the merge commit for osb-u4 and osb-u2, one heavy run at a time, covering the full available W and reaching its end with no OOM; record each peak RSS (MemoryMax is set from that peak plus a margin); exit 0 is required, and any `RuntimeError` ("ns_top1 on a released mint") or a scope OOM is a FAIL; read counts only. (4) The notebook entry carries the commit, `code.sha256`, the peak RSS of both books and the disclosure. (5) The twins later run at the same pin, by full replay and `--compare` (ADD2 (a)2, unchanged). **Required disclosure (QP on PR #1432, verbatim on this record, the notebook entry, every board note, the 5-day report and any proposal):** "U4/U2 bounded state (PR #1432): decisions, counts and mark records are md5-equal to the previous code path (bounded=False) on synthetic fixtures in block-time order. On the real input this rests on the arena tip writer/reader contract at 916b3275 (rows released in slot order; out-of-order refetches become gaps). It was not measured on the tape. If a pool's prints were ever out of block-time order by tens of minutes, U2's price-at-or-before lookups could differ from the previous code (QP synthetic probes: 0 of 4,200 lookups with 5% of prints up to 30 min late; 39 of 4,200 with 5% up to 2 h late). U4 matched in all 408 hours with rows up to 2 h late. A create row repeated more than 8 days after the first, with different fields, would keep that mint up to 8 days longer than the previous code (a true repeat behaves as before)." Paper only; this is an open simulation, not a gate read, and no book has cleared the promotion gate. No figure exists.
- **Disclosures (verbatim on every figure, board note, 5-day report and proposal).** (1) The §4 disclosure of the ruling, copied exactly with its brackets filled: "U4 has no measured number of any kind before this book: the rule and every constant were fixed a priori in QP-RULING-1116-u4-u2-p81-forward-specs before any of its data was read. The nearest library priors are dead or explored-no-survivor (A45/E13 overhang vetoes, dead). This book's figures are forward paper from S at 4.085 s entry with honest costs; they are not a gate read and not out-of-sample evidence of an edge. Treat any good number as shrunk (best of <n_books_watched> rows). Decode equality of post-2026-11-15 rows for the fields used is not shown." (1a) "NS is a tape proxy: transfers are invisible, so 'never sold' includes tokens moved to other wallets." (2) ADD1 A1 disclosure, copied exactly: "Strict feed-gap exclusion: any data_loss gap between decision and exit removes the trade (engine default; gap-in-hold mode cannot be combined with the 19-slot latency). Exclusion odds rise with hold length, so early exits (stops, takes, share-fall) are over-represented among priced trades; excluded <n> of <N> entries (<timed>/<requested>)." (3) Warm-up disclosure (ruling 0.3): "warm-up W = [2026-11-03T12:00Z, S), decisions off, no outcome code path; state (balances / prices / registries) starts at 2026-11-03T12:00Z; pools or mints with history before 2026-11-03T12:00Z are excluded and counted: <n>". (4) The 9 rows (3 stakes x 3 latencies) of this book count in n_books_watched and the best-of-N sentence; the 160,000 column does not. 0.10 and 0.25: "model; no live fills for this rule"; 0.5: "model only, extrapolated; not for steering".
- **Read.** Mechanical, ruling section 0.8: the 5-day look L1 = S + 5 d (extended once to S + 10 d if n < 10), deciding cell stake 0.10, entry 19 slots, 505,000 per send, both fail legs; GO iff total net SOL > 0, total after dropping the top 3 trades > 0, strictly more positive than non-positive complete days, excluded <= 20% of forward entries, zero rows decided before S, and the book's mean SOL per trade above the random-pick book's under both legs; otherwise NO-GO. Not a gate read, never pooled with real fills, never part of a later read's data; a GO licenses only a small DEC-035 lane start under its own amendment (QP + reviewer at the exact head, owner's word, Am.9 section 3 rule). Winner's curse and the promotion gate are unchanged. Paper only. Nothing here says U4 makes money. No book has cleared the promotion gate.

**Amendment 9 book-list record, 2026-11-16: osb-u2 (U2 open simulation).** Under §4 ("Later books ... start under this amendment"), one more open simulation book, not in design §1, and a different kind from the FLOOR books: U2 (cohort-residual relative strength: at 00 / 06 / 12 / 18 UTC, pools aged [24 h, 168 h) after migration with quote reserve >= 150 SOL, 24 h return minus its age-bucket median, trade only if breadth > 0.40, buy the top 3 residuals > 0). A create row repeated more than 8 days after the first, with different fields, would keep that mint up to 8 days longer than the previous code (a true repeat behaves as before)." Paper only; this is an open simulation, not a gate read, and no book has cleared the promotion gate. No figure exists.
- **Disclosures (verbatim on every figure, board note, 5-day report and proposal).** (1) The §4 disclosure of the ruling, copied exactly with its brackets filled: "U2 has no measured number of any kind before this book: the rule and every constant were fixed a priori in QP-RULING-1116-u4-u2-p81-forward-specs before any of its data was read. The nearest library priors are dead or explored-no-survivor (survivors-1, day-sigma-1, explored-no-survivor). This book's figures are forward paper from S at 4.085 s entry with honest costs; they are not a gate read and not out-of-sample evidence of an edge. Treat any good number as shrunk (best of <n_books_watched> rows). Decode equality of post-2026-11-15 rows for the fields used is not shown."  (2) ADD1 A1 disclosure, copied exactly: "Strict feed-gap exclusion: any data_loss gap between decision and exit removes the trade (engine default; gap-in-hold mode cannot be combined with the 19-slot latency). Exclusion odds rise with hold length, so early exits (stops, takes, share-fall) are over-represented among priced trades; excluded <n> of <N> entries (<timed>/<requested>)." (3) Warm-up disclosure (ruling 0.3): "warm-up W = [2026-11-03T12:00Z, S), decisions off, no outcome code path; state (balances / prices / registries) starts at 2026-11-03T12:00Z; pools or mints with history before 2026-11-03T12:00Z are excluded and counted: <n>". (4) The 9 rows (3 stakes x 3 latencies) of this book count in n_books_watched and the best-of-N sentence; the 160,000 column does not. 0.10 and 0.25: "model; no live fills for this rule"; 0.5: "model only, extrapolated; not for steering".
- **Read.** Mechanical, ruling section 0.8: the 5-day look L1 = S + 5 d (extended once to S + 10 d if n < 10), deciding cell stake 0.10, entry 19 slots, 505,000 per send, both fail legs; GO iff total net SOL > 0, total after dropping the top 3 trades > 0, strictly more positive than non-positive complete days, excluded <= 20% of forward entries, zero rows decided before S, and the book's mean SOL per trade above the random-pick book's under both legs; otherwise NO-GO. Not a gate read, never pooled with real fills, never part of a later read's data; a GO licenses only a small DEC-035 lane start under its own amendment (QP + reviewer at the exact head, owner's word, Am.9 section 3 rule). Winner's curse and the promotion gate are unchanged. Paper only. Nothing here says U2 makes money. No book has cleared the promotion gate.

## Amendment 10 (2026-11-14): floor-lane-1 executor key `rpc_rps` 4.0
**Status: DRAFT until merge. NOT installed. It authorises nothing until the owner's own word and a Helm install.** Written 2026-11-14T10:15Z (`date -u`) for manager23, at main e5fd255b. Amendment 10 was the next free number (Amendment 9 and its Erratum 1 are the last). Records only: no code, host or live file is touched. Nothing here says FLOOR or any variant makes money, and no promotion-gate threshold changes.

Sources (host paths under `/data/mal/hunt-1008/ops/reports/2026-11-14/`): `floor-buy-latency-trace.md` (cause, file:line); `pr1384-qp.md` (QP VERDICT OK at b4ef43f1, and its re-verdict OK at ee3f9704, which keeps the enabling list unchanged); `pr1384-reviewer.md` and `pr1384-reviewer-r2.md` (REVIEWER APPROVE at ee3f9704); PR #1384 (merged e5fd255b43ddfda09eb7cf49f6477cda0bf08797; QP OK and REVIEWER APPROVE at ee3f970428b1e3e2944d93f94274636176216e6e); code at main: `tools/floor_lane_executor.py` (`parse_rpc_rps`, `build_live_port`, the start print `rpc_rps=`).

**1. What changes.** One top-level key in the executor's live config, and nothing else: `"rpc_rps": 4.0` (a JSON number).
- The key is **TOP-LEVEL** `rpc_rps`. `lane.rpc_rps` and `lane.rps` are refused as unknown lane keys, so the executor will not start (it does not fall back to 2 rps). A top-level `rps` is silently ignored by the lane executor (only the arena bridge's own `main`, `tools/arena_live_bridge.py:1685`, reads it, and the FLOOR lane never runs that `main`), so a config that writes `rps` for `rpc_rps` changes nothing. The install check is the start line (item 7).
- Default (key absent) = 2.0 = today's behaviour (`RPC_RPS_DEFAULT`, equal to `pe.MAX_RPS`; a test pins the equality).
- Code ceiling 5.0 (`LIVE_MAX_RPS`, `RPC_RPS_MAX`). **This amendment authorises 4.0 only.** Any other value, including 5.0, is a new amendment.
- The value is validated at start (`parse_rpc_rps`); a value outside [2, 5], a bool, string, NaN or infinity is refused and the executor does not start.

**2. Why.**
- **Measured** (manager job #2702 on mal-fast-0, 2026-11-14, timings only, all 12 live buys): bridge decision to `send_ms` 2,048 to 2,105 ms on 11 buys and 2,739 ms on one; decision slot to landed slot 17 to 26, median 19; sells, decision to send, 2 to 54 ms. First recon rerun (job #2699): live landing-row times 6,118 and 7,251 ms on the two 0.10 trades.
- **Cause** (code reading, `floor-buy-latency-trace.md`; derived, not measured): the lane's RPC client is a token bucket at 2.0 rps (0.5 s spacing; `floor_lane_executor.py:674` before #1384, `probe_executor.py:58`), and before the bridge stamps `send_ms` it makes 5 serial non-priority reads (pool account, pool snapshot as 2 calls, wallet balance, mint account), so 4 gaps of 0.5 s plus read latency is 2.05 to 2.1 s. Nothing waits for the paper fill. The sell path skips these reads (warm snapshot, cached blockhash, priority calls), which is why sells send in 2 to 54 ms.
- **What the simulator assumes:** 1.9 s from decision to landed. The live buys land at 17 to 26 slots (median 19), which is later than that assumption.
- **Expected effect:** about 1.0 s earlier `send_ms` at 4 rps (QP: "about 1.0 s on `send_ms`"; 1.2 s at 5 rps). **This is arithmetic from the limiter, not measured.** No claim is made that the lane makes money or that the change improves P&L.

**3. What does NOT change.** Stake, `max_open`, budget, daily stop, wallet floor, mint screen, the 5 s stale-decision limit (bridge `_presend_refusal` and executor `stale_signal`), the A3 pause, the STOP files, the pre-buy gate, the order of the pre-send reads, the priority contexts (`PRIORITY_BURST`), the host (never restarted), and every §2 limit. QP: the key "reaches only `LimitedRpc.interval = 1/min(rps, 5.0)`". The priority slack is `(burst-1)*interval`, so at 4 rps it is 0.75 s instead of 1.5 s in wall time; it never permits a call that would otherwise be refused. QP, quoted: "429 or RPC error on a pre-send read: fails closed (behaviour already on main, unchanged here)": the entry is refused (`no_mint`, `pool_unreadable:<label>`, `balance_unreadable`, mint screen `fetch_ok` false), "No path sends without its read's check", and "A `timeout` or 5xx on `sendTransaction` is retried inside `LimitedRpc`, but it re-sends the identical signed bytes (same signature), after the write-ahead. It cannot buy twice. This is on main already and does not depend on the rate."

**4. Risk.**
- **HTTP 429.** The RPC plan is shared with the listener and the tip follower. The follower's own rate is `--rps 15 --fetch-workers 8` (its unit line, as given by the manager from job #2703; not looked up by the drafter). A higher lane rate makes a 429 more likely; backoff waits (1 s, 2 s, 4 s) are unchanged. A 7 s backoff runs past the 5 s decision age and the buy is refused as stale.
- **429 retries are logged nowhere today.** `LimitedRpc` does not log retries and `calls` is not exported. Only retry-exhausted failures show, as refusal labels. The Helius dashboard request and 429 figures for the key are the only direct view. A retry counter would be a separate T1 code PR.
- A refusal from a 429 costs one missed entry, never a wrong send.

**5. "It worked" measure, fixed before the first buy** (QP enabling list item 2). Window: from the executor start line that ends `rpc_rps=4.0` to the 10th live buy, all under that one executor tree and config. Another executor install or config change inside the window ends it; the count restarts at the next start line, and no second install is made inside a window. A live buy is a bridge `buy_sent` row with `would_send` false. The rows are read by a manager MiScusi job on mal-fast-0 that prints only the fields named below (timings, counts and order ids; no price, fill amount, P&L or exit field) and is notebooked.
- **(a) The class field is the bridge `decision_to_send_ms` of each live buy (the field of job #2702; baseline 2,048 to 2,105 ms on 11 of 12, 2,739 ms once).** Median of the window: at or below 1,300 ms = **worked**; above 1,700 ms = **no effect**; in between = keep and report. On **no effect**, the key is removed at the next executor install. Keeping it needs a new amendment. Arithmetic expectation (not measured): about 1,050 to 1,100 ms. The executor fields `ms_decision_to_send` and `first_send_ms - decision_t_ms` are reported beside it (median and range) with no class, because their pre-change baseline was not measured (the trace report puts it at about 2.5 to 3.0 s, derived, not measured).
- **(b) Slots, reported only:** `landed_slot - decision_slot` and `state_slot - decision_slot`, median and range, beside the pre-change median 19 (range 17 to 26).
- **(c) RPC-error refusals (rollback trigger).** One rule: count, in the window,
  - bridge `entry_refused` rows whose `why` is `no_mint`, `balance_unreadable`, `close_blocked_mint:fetch_failed`, `close_blocked_mint:screen_error`, `stale_decision`, or `pool_unreadable:` followed by `rate_limited`, `server_error`, `timeout`, `network`, `bad_json`, `http_<code>` or `rpc_error<...>`;
  - bridge `buy_port_refused` rows whose `label` is `stale_signal`, `balance_unreadable`, one of the listed RPC words, or `build_error:` followed by one of them.

  More than 1 such row in the window = roll back. `no_mint` and the stale words can have non-RPC causes; counting them errs toward rollback, which is the safe side. Each counted row is listed by word and count. The other `pool_unreadable:` causes (`no_canonical_pool`, `bad_mint`, fetch_snapshot strings) are not counted.
  - **Candidate** (the denominator, reported beside the count, not part of the trigger): an entry row that passed `entry_refusal` and so reached the first pre-send read (`mint_for_pool`).
  - **Pre-change baseline** (reported beside the count, not part of the trigger): the same job counts the same words and candidates over the 12 buys at 2 rps (the job #2702 period). The ask fills the figures; this amendment does not state them.
- **(d) End point.** If the 10th live buy has not come 7 days after the start line, the measure is read on the live buys so far if there are at least 5, with the same classes, and marked "n < 10". With fewer than 5 it is INSUFFICIENT: the key stays, (c) keeps running, and the read is repeated each 7 days until 10.
- **(e) Helius credits** of the lane for the first 3 days after the install, recorded (CLAUDE.md "Credits"), with the dashboard's 429 count for the key if it shows one.
- No P&L number is part of this measure. None of (a) to (e) says the lane makes money.

**6. Install preconditions.** Before the ask, all hold, and the manager records them in the state file:
- (a) QP OK and REVIEWER APPROVE at the exact head of PR #1384 (met: ee3f9704, merged e5fd255b) and of this amendment's PR, and both merged.
- (b) Unit tests at the merge sha. QP at ee3f9704: 395 passed (the new file's 35 plus the 7 existing executor, bridge and probe files, `MAL_ARENA_TEST=1`). Reviewer at ee3f9704: 638 passed and 60 subtests passed (new file plus the wider set). The install ask records the same set re-run at the install tree's commit.
- (c) **No md5 decision-equivalence replay is owed.** The executor makes no decisions and the change is execution-only: the port is live-only, a DRY replay builds `DryPort` and never reads the key, and the decision host is untouched. QP (enabling list item 5): "The CLAUDE.md runner rule is met by tests, on the B6 precedent" and "The change is execution-only, the port is live-only". The precedent is the DEC-035 text at line 619 (Am.8 B6): "Amendments 4, 5 and 6 installed executor code changes on QP OK, REVIEWER APPROVE and the unit tests at the merge sha, with no replay proof". Cited tests: `test_default_builds_limiter_with_exactly_2`, `test_key_changes_nothing_else`, `test_run_passes_key_to_build_live_port`, and the 360 existing tests.
- (d) **The owner's own word** in the Helm chat for this install, quoted in the install record. New code and config are outside the owner's 2026-11-13 restart pre-approval (see Amendment 8 "Install").
- (e) **Stops and positions.** The lane STOP file and the wallet-wide STOP and HALT files are absent at the ask. Lane cash and the number of open positions are recorded (an executor restart empties the marks, Amendment 4 item 6).
- (f) **Rollback config pre-staged:** "the config of the running executor at the time of the ask", without the key, with its sha256 recorded in the ask. Setting the key changes the config bytes, so the install needs a new pin (QP: neither executor nor bridge hashes its config).
- (g) **Install set, pinned.** Executor-only restart (`mal-floor-lane-executor`; never the host), outside the 11-15 tip hand-off window [2026-11-15T05:00:00Z, 2026-11-15T09:00:00Z) and its confirmations (Amendment 7 Timing, line 441), bundled with the next executor install if one is due (the HELM-ASK-1108-1 reset window 2026-11-16..11-21). The code is main at e5fd255b: the one changed execution blob is `tools/floor_lane_executor.py` 1807db6aa327941c33a7b931522b00ae5c0fce3b (+24/-4 against the installed pin 9c52f4fc). `git diff --stat 9c52f4fc e5fd255b` over `tools/floor_lane_executor.py`, `tools/arena_live_bridge.py`, `tools/floor_arena_executor.py`, `tools/probe_live.py`, `tools/probe_executor.py` and `tools/h5_arena_executor.py` shows `tools/floor_lane_executor.py` only (1 file, 24 insertions, 4 deletions), and the same diff over `tools/forge/dsl_lane_executor.py` and `tools/h5_lane_executor.py` shows nothing. The ask states: code diff = that one blob; config diff = that one key `"rpc_rps": 4.0`. A tree at any later commit is a new ask. The ask fills these fields: new executor tree path and commit; new config sha256; pre-staged rollback config sha256 and the tree it runs on.
- (h) **Rollback word.** The owner's install word covers the rollback to the pre-staged config (Amendment 7 pattern, line 432), so a trigger of item 5 needs no second owner word. The rollback target is tree + config sha256 as named in (g): the same tree with the pre-staged key-absent config, or the installed pin 9c52f4fc tree with that config (equivalent once the key is absent).

**7. After the install.**
- The executor start line must end `rpc_rps=4.0` (QP: "the install check is the start line `rpc_rps=<value>`"). The manager records the line, and the stop and start instants go to LAB_STATE, the state file and the MiScusi notebook (the Amendment 8 part B install-record practice). A start line showing `rpc_rps=2.0` means the key was not read: report to Helm.
- The recon structure check is re-run (executor redeploy). Reconciliation windows that straddle the install instant are reported with that instant marked (S1 entry slip and S4 mix 2 rps and 4 rps trades); no band constant changes.
- The item 5 measure is read at the end of its window (item 5 (d)) and notebooked.
- The open-sim reconciliation band S4 is **not** the yardstick (QP): its landing time is the log row (which carries about 2.5 to 3.0 s of poll and limiter-debt lag that is not chain time), and its slot leg is calibrated at 0.4 s steps while slots are about 215 ms. Whether to recalibrate it is a separate QP ruling on the reconciliation tool's PR (Amendment 9 §3). Until then S4 stays as written and is not loosened for this step.

**Rollback.** Remove the key and restart the executor only, through Helm, using the pre-staged config of item 6 (f), under the word of item 6 (h). No code rollback is needed: with the key absent the code behaves as before. Triggers: item 5 (a) **no effect** (at the next executor install), item 5 (c) more than 1 counted row in the window, or any `send_error` alert attributed to the rate.

**8. Scope.** Later steps (a priority context for the pre-send reads, removing duplicate reads, a warm blockhash) are separate code PRs and separate amendments.

**Unchanged.** §2 (every limit, the STOP files, keys), §3 to §7, the Amendment 6 gate and its count, Amendments 1 to 9 and their errata, EXP-029 Erratum 1g rule 3 (a later registration splits trades by host sha and executor config, using the install instants recorded here), and the promotion gate. No book has cleared the promotion gate.

## Amendment 10, Erratum 1 (2026-11-14): the simulator-latency sentence
In force from its merge. Written 2026-11-14T11:40Z (`date -u`) for manager23. Records only; wording correction. Source: the QP ruling `/data/mal/hunt-1008/ops/QP-RULING-1114-osb-latency-twin.md` (sha256 prefix cd2bfde2), "Verdict on questions 1 and 2". As with Amendment 9 Erratum 1, the amended sentence is not edited in place; this block follows Amendment 10 and controls where they differ.

**Wrong sentence (Amendment 10, "Why"):** "**What the simulator assumes:** 1.9 s from decision to landed. The live buys land at 17 to 26 slots (median 19), which is later than that assumption."

**Corrected statement.** The strict books do not price a flat 1.9 s landing. The engine indexes tape slots and converts seconds to slots with the measured seconds per slot, and it adds a fixed feed delay d = 7 slots (`cfg.feed_delay_slots`, held in slots, measured once) before the 1.9 s latency. At about 215 ms a slot the strict entry k(1.9) = ceil(1.9 / 0.215) = 9 slots, so the strict books land an entry 7 + 9 = 16 slots (about 3.44 s) after the decision. Live: median 19 slots (about 4.09 s), slowest 26 (about 5.59 s). The simulator is therefore about 3 slots (about 0.65 s) early on the median and up to 10 slots (about 2.15 s) early on the slowest buy, not about 2 s early across the board. If Amendment 10 works as its arithmetic expects (about 14 slots), live would land slightly earlier than the simulator's 16.

**Measured and derived.** Measured: job #2702 timings, 12 live buys (decision slot to landed slot 17 to 26, median 19). Derived from code reading, not measured: the 16 slots (QP reading of `ArenaEngine.sps()`, `k_slots`, the engine's `cfg.feed_delay_slots` = 7) and the 0.215 s per slot (A3 record 213 to 215 ms/slot). The 14-slot figure is arithmetic, not measured.

**What it changes.** Only the sentence above. It changes no condition, limit, measure or install step of Amendment 10: item 5's class thresholds are on the bridge `decision_to_send_ms`, not on a simulator comparison. The recon's S4 band is not a valid yardstick as written (already stated in Amendment 10 item 7). Latency pricing twins of the simulator are in Amendment 9 Erratum 2. Nothing here says FLOOR or any variant makes money. No book has cleared the promotion gate.

The `funded_lamports`, `wallet_floor_count_held_once` and fee reserve values are as Am.6 recorded them for the Am.5 config; the manager re-reads the live config at the ask by the manager's own job and the ask states any difference. The shared `LANE_DAILY_MAX_LAMPORTS`, `DSL_LANE_*`, `h5_lane_executor.py` constants and `floor_arena_executor.py` C0 pins are not touched (Am.6 item 4).

**Wallet floor with open exposure at 0.25 (arithmetic at the HANDOFF reading; recomputed at the ask).** Balance 2,952,437,180 lamports at 2026-11-15T19:31Z, with 2 positions open at 0.101673840 SOL basis each (their stakes already out of the balance). Held-once floor: a landed buy's stake has left the balance, so the next open needs 250,000,000 + 160,000 (send fee) + 2,100,000 (rent) + (n + 1) x 1,600,000 (sell reserve, n = positions open) + 750,000,000. With n = 2 that is 1,007,060,000; each further landed 0.25 open takes about 252,260,000 from the balance and adds 1,600,000 to the need. Further 0.25 opens the floor alone admits: (2,952,437,180 - 1,007,060,000) / (252,260,000 + 1,600,000) = 7.66, and the floor counts the first open too (each further open must pass at its own n), so the floor alone admits **8** further opens: the 8th further open sees a balance of 1,186,617,180 against a need of 1,018,260,000 and passes, and a 9th would see 934,357,180 against 1,019,860,000 and fail. Budget side (committed loss, realized floored at 0): the 2 open 0.10 positions count 2 x (100,000,000 + 3,860,000) = 207,720,000 and each new 0.25 open 253,860,000: (2,100,000,000 - 207,720,000) / 253,860,000 = 7.45, so **7** further now, and the pre-send arm (Am.3 full-wipe bound, realized +10,363,342) also gives 7; 8 once the 0.10 positions have closed, which holds only while realized loss stays within 69,120,000 lamports (2,100,000,000 - 8 x 253,860,000; Am.6: 8 x 253,860,000 = 2,030,880,000 <= 2,100,000,000 < 9 x 253,860,000). The budget binds first at 7 and the wallet floor at 8; the wallet floor binds first if the balance at the ask is lower (about one open per 0.254 SOL). These are structural counts, not a forecast of entries (about 2 a day in the HANDOFF window). **In plain numbers:** the lane can still lose about 2.11 SOL (the 2.10 SOL budget plus lifetime realized +0.0104) before the budget stop, from a wallet of 2.952 SOL (about 71%), above a 0.75 SOL floor; 7 further 0.25 opens plus the 2 open 0.10 positions put about 1.98 SOL of stake out at once; and one UTC day can lose more than 0.50 SOL because the daily stop is checked before an entry only. The worst-case bound is Am.6's: lifetime lane loss bounded by 2.10 SOL; the wallet floor 0.75 SOL; the daily stop 0.50 SOL is checked before an entry only (Am.6 Consequences).

**What the evidence is, and is not.** Ten closed trades are thinner than the 20 that Am.6 asked for, and at 0.10 they say nothing about fills or impact at 0.25 (a stake 2.5 times larger; round-trip impact about 6.25 times in SOL per trade, Am.6 "Price impact", model figures not fills). The November paper evidence at 0.25 (Am.6 Errata 2 and 3) is in-sample and not a gate read: with the 29 gap-excluded positions priced through the holes, 0.25 flat uncapped n 79 is +0.001194 SOL with ex-top-3 -0.506554, K = 16 is +0.412001 with ex-top-3 -0.095747, and the post-deploy ex-top-3 is negative at every cap. Live: 12 round trips total +0.010363342 SOL and, without the one take-profit of 0.018778152 SOL, -0.008414810 SOL (HANDOFF section 3, QP arithmetic). What the manager's profit map (`ops/reports/2026-11-15/profit-map.md`) draws from the same numbers, in plain words: the all-entries mean is about zero (+0.001194 SOL over 79 positions is about +0.000015 SOL per trade at 0.25); the live trigger rate is about 2 entries a day against 8.8 in-sample, so FLOOR at 0.25 is worth about 0.00 to 0.04 SOL a day (about 0 to 4 USD); and profit rides on 1 to 2 take-profit trades. The step multiplies per-trade exposure by 2.5 (model impact by about 6.25) on a book whose all-entries mean is about 0 and whose ex-top-3 is negative: it buys fills evidence at size and is not a step toward a measured edge. The book A recon is not decision-grade (item 4). This amendment records what the owner's approval is conditional on and turns none of it into an edge claim.

**Post-install check and rollback (Am.6, unchanged).** `tools.floor_fills_vs_model` at the 10th closed trade at stake 0.25; a "no" writes the lane STOP file and the manager asks Helm for the pre-staged rollback config (item 10). Rollback is the config only, executor-only restart, never the host.

**EXP-029.** No registration, erratum or FAIL-end event is created (Erratum 1g rule 3 unchanged). A later registration splits trades by executor config at the install instant, which the install record gives.

**Unchanged.** Am.6 in full except "20" in its install gate as set above; Am.10 (the item 5 window and its rollback rule); §2 limits other than the Am.6 values; the promotion gate; the seals; the A3 pauses; the H3 cap and every recon band; the clocks of HANDOFF section 5. A stake above 0.25 SOL, or a budget above 2.10 SOL, still needs a new amendment with the owner's word and a price-impact estimate (§6).

No book has cleared the promotion gate. Nothing here says FLOOR makes money.
