## Amendment 12 (2026-11-17): floor-lane-1 lifetime budget 2.10 to 4.00 SOL, with an owner wallet top-up; stake, daily stop and open cap unchanged (DRAFT)

**Status: DRAFT until merge. NOT installed. It authorises nothing until QP OK and REVIEWER APPROVE at the exact head, the merge, the owner's word quoted below, every precondition below met and recorded, and a Helm install.** Drafted 2026-11-17 (`date -u` about 21:4xZ; revised about 22:3xZ after QP NOT OK and REVIEWER CHANGES at 9f386677; revised 2026-11-18 about 01:0xZ (and again about 01:0xZ after REVIEWER CHANGES at a981fefe and at 3013a29c; the commit times are the record) with the owner's word, the 2.0 SOL top-up and the precondition 2 result) by manager24 at main 784e8aea. No book has cleared the promotion gate, and nothing here says FLOOR makes money. No gate threshold, seal or clock changes.

**Decider.** Vaan (owner).

**The owner's word (MiScusi Console chat, 2026-11-18, read about 01:0xZ).** The manager's update before it (2026-11-18 about 00:5xZ) set out the two options in a table, "4.00 budget + 1.5 SOL (my recommendation)" against "3.50 budget + 1.0 SOL", with "Positions open at once 15 (the sim holds 15) / 13", "Most the lane can lose in total about 4.15 SOL / about 3.65 SOL" and "Wallet value at cost about 4.8 SOL / about 4.3 SOL", and asked: "Which option you want. Approving the 4.00 budget now lets me install it as soon as tonight's exits are clean, and the capital lifts the limit to 15 whenever it lands." and "Roughly how much SOL and when, so I can recompute the limits." The owner answered, verbatim:

> "I'm getting 2 sol in asap, hopefully today but probably tomorrow. 2 sol is just easier, might as well put the 0.5 in there, you can use it but you don't have to.  if it's better then that is great. Keep working on the other papers, and periodically check on what the pnl is looking like, we don't have to only look at it once it's done. Keep working, good stuff so far, we're making progress, let's keep it up and we'll be making more money in no time."

The manager reads this as: the recommended row, budget 4.00, with a top-up of 2.0 SOL instead of 1.5 (the extra 0.5 is the owner's choice of round sum; "you can use it but you don't have to"), on the loss figures shown to the owner. At budget 4.00 the extra 0.5 SOL adds no open slot (the budget binds at 15, table below); it is wallet buffer above the floor. The risk table below is restated for 2.0. This is the owner's decision word for this amendment; the install still needs the owner's own word in the Helm chat (precondition 8, Am.10 item 6 (d)).

**What this amendment changes, and only this.**
1. `budget_lamports` (Am.3 full-wipe bound and `committed_loss_stop`) 2,100,000,000 to **4,000,000,000**. This is the code ceiling `LANE_BUDGET_MAX_LAMPORTS` merged in PR #1439; no new code ceiling.
2. **Executor tree pinned to commit `5fc473b5256cdd1d0612fec6d460cb4a19d5bf1a`** (the #1439 merge), in place of `e5fd255b`. `git diff --stat e5fd255b 5fc473b5` over the executor's code (`tools/floor_lane_executor.py`, `tools/arena_live_bridge.py`, `tools/floor_arena_executor.py`, `tools/probe_live.py`, `tools/probe_executor.py`, `tools/h5_arena_executor.py`, `tools/h5_lane_executor.py`, `tools/forge/dsl_lane_executor.py`, `tools/arena_declog.py`, `tools/hv4_c0_parity.py`, `tools/hv4_live_c0.py`, `tools/arena_paper.py`) shows two files: `tools/arena_live_bridge.py` +147/-5 (blob 7ea4fb4c to d01e93a8) and `tools/floor_lane_executor.py` +28/-6 (blob 1807db6a to b3382217) (`git diff --numstat`). They carry #1409 (927d140e) and #1410 (d8dad7b9), opt-in batched pre-send reads and per-stage time stamps, and #1439 (the ceiling). A tree at any other commit is a new ask (Am.10 (g) pattern).
3. The config does **not** set `presend_batch`, so the pre-send reads stay serial as now (absent = false, `parse_presend_batch`). What still changes with the key absent: every live buy row carries the new `stage_ms` and `exec_stage_ms` stamps, and the start line gains ` presend_batch=false`. Turning `presend_batch` on is a later, separate step with its own measure window, never in this install.
4. The owner adds SOL to the lane wallet (2.0 SOL, per the owner's word above). This is the owner's own transfer, not a config value, and it may land before or after the install: the wallet-floor check reads the free balance by `getBalance` before each send (`presend_balance` falls back to `balance()` with `presend_batch` absent, `tools/arena_live_bridge.py` at `5fc473b5`), so a top-up that lands after the install takes effect with no restart and no config change. The wallet floor (0.75 SOL, held once) is unchanged.

Unchanged: stake 0.25 SOL; daily stop 0.50 SOL (`FLOOR_LANE_DAILY_MAX_LAMPORTS`, marked P&L, checked before an entry only); `max_open` 16; reserve 3,860,000 per position; `rpc_rps` 4.0; rule `l1-floor-v2`; the decision host (never restarted); A3 pauses; every recon band; the promotion gate; §2 limits other than the budget. It overrides §6 for floor-lane-1 only, as Amendments 4 to 6 and 11 did.

**Why (counts from the manager's hourlies; paper figures labelled).**
- With 8 positions open at 0.25 both budget checks refuse a new open (hourlies #2979 to #2985, 2026-11-17): the full-wipe pre-send headroom is 0.241314980 SOL against 0.25 + 9 x 0.00386 = 0.28474 SOL needed at n = 8, and the `committed_loss_stop` room is 2.10 - 8 x 0.25386 = 0.06912 SOL against 0.25386.
- The open-sim recon (book A, paper, no limit layer, window to 2026-11-17T20:00Z) counts 8 `sim_only` rows with reason `executor:lane_budget` on 2026-11-17: signals book A took and live did not. One has closed in book A, osb-a-00021, at its take-profit: **+0.5503 SOL flat / +0.5213 pressure at 0.25 (paper model; SIM OFF, recon FAIL; not decision-grade)**. Seven are still open. Tracker: `ops/manager24/missed-tracker.py`.
- On the one 0.25 trade closed both live and in book A (osb-a-00022), live netted +0.129651905 SOL and book A +0.089570443 SOL.

**Capacity at 0.25** (reserve 3,860,000; budget slots = floor(budget / 253,860,000), which assumes lifetime realized >= 0 and so is conservative: each 0.254 SOL of realized loss removes one slot; wallet floor held once: the next open needs 250,000,000 + 160,000 + 2,100,000 + (n + 1) x 1,600,000 + 750,000,000 of free balance, and each landed open takes about 252,260,000; from the free balance 1,286,736,501 lamports with n = 8 open at hourly #2985; recomputed at the ask with the realized figure, the balance and n then):

| budget | +0 SOL | +0.5 | +1.0 | +1.5 | +2.0 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| 2.10 (now) | 8 | 8 | 8 | 8 | 8 |
| 3.00 | 10 | 11 | 11 | 11 | 11 |
| 3.50 | 10 | 12 | 13 | 13 | 13 |
| 4.00 | 10 | 12 | 14 | 15 | 15 |

The budget alone (no top-up) admits at most 10 open, because the wallet floor binds first. At 4.00 with +1.5 or +2.0 SOL: 15 open at once, 3.75 SOL of stake out (about 3.78 SOL basis with rent and fees). These are structural counts, not a forecast of entries.

**Price impact (§6).** Unchanged per trade: the stake stays 0.25, so Am.6 "Price impact" stands (round-trip about 0.007 SOL per trade, model figures). Positions are in distinct pools (one open per pool), so more concurrent positions add exposure, not impact on each other. They are not independent: a program redeploy, an A3 burst or a market-wide dip can hit all open positions at once, so losses can come together.

**Risk in plain words (figures the owner's word covers; recomputed at the ask).** Lifetime realized at hourly #2985 is +0.154705700 SOL (0.241314980 + 2.013390720 - 2.10).

| | 4.00 budget + 2.0 SOL (the owner's choice) | 4.00 budget + 1.5 SOL (as shown to the owner) | 3.50 budget + 1.0 SOL (as shown to the owner) |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| Most the lane can still lose from the ask (budget + lifetime realized) | about 4.15 SOL | about 4.15 SOL | about 3.65 SOL |
| Wallet at cost after the top-up (free balance + open basis + top-up) | about 5.30 SOL | about 4.80 SOL | about 4.30 SOL |
| Share of the wallet that can be lost | about 78% | about 86% | about 85% |
| Positions open at once | up to 15 | up to 15 | up to 13 |
| What one UTC day can lose | nearly all of it: the daily stop is checked before an entry only, so the bound is min(remaining room, 0.50 + 15 x 0.2539 = 4.31) | the same, 4.31 | the same rule, min(remaining room, 0.50 + 13 x 0.2539 = 3.80) |

The 2.0 SOL column changes only the wallet figures: the loss bound is set by the budget (4.00 plus lifetime realized), so the extra 0.5 SOL lowers the share at risk and adds no exposure. Before the top-up lands, the budget alone admits at most 10 open (wallet floor), so the most at stake at once is 10 x 0.2539 = 2.54 SOL; the lifetime bound is the same 4.15 SOL, limited in practice by the wallet floor until the top-up: the wallet at cost before the top-up is about 3.30 SOL (free 1.286736501 + open basis 2.013390720 at drafting), so with the 0.75 SOL floor held, about 2.55 SOL is what can actually be lost before the top-up lands.

The marked daily stop (0.50 SOL) halts new entries on a fall of about 11 to 13% of the marks with 15 open (13.3% of stake; about 10.6% once the drop at the fill is counted). The 0.75 SOL wallet floor always stays. Evidence is thin: live at 0.25 is 1 closed trade before tonight's time stops; the November paper all-entries mean at 0.25 is about zero with a negative ex-top-3 (Am.6 Errata 2 and 3); the missed-signal figure above rests on one paper take-profit. This step buys more live fills at the same size; it is not a measured edge.

The ask quotes the owner's word, lists each precondition 1 to 7 as met or not met, and states the recon state as it stands (Am.11 install record lesson). Am.10 item 6 (d): new code needs the owner's own word in the Helm chat for this install. (QP conditions at a981fefe:) C1: the ask states budget 4.00 SOL, the lifetime loss bound of about 4.15 SOL and the one-day bound of about 4.31 SOL, and the owner's Helm-chat word names the 4.00 budget and the 4.15 SOL bound; that word is quoted in the install record. C2: the ask confirms that `budget_lamports` is the only changed value, so `funded_lamports` and `wallet_floor_lamports` are unchanged and the floor stays 750,000,000 lamports.

**Post-install check.** The executor start line must end exactly `stake=250000000 rpc_rps=4.0 presend_batch=false`. Any other tail (`presend_batch=true`, or `rpc_rps` not 4.0) is a failed install and the rollback below runs at once. A third Am.10 item 5 window opens at that start line (before the install the manager updates `am10-measure.sh` to open window 3 at that line, matching the `rpc_rps=4.0` field, not the line's ending, and a manager job confirms the script catches the new line); the first live buy after it must land with no new refusal word in `entry_refused` / `buy_port_refused`. The missed-signal tracker keeps running; it is paper and decides nothing.

**Rollback (one target, in this order).** For any Am.12 failure (failed start line, an item 5 (c) trigger in window 3 (more than 1 counted row), a `send_error` attributed to the new code, or an owner stop): remove the new drop-in, daemon-reload, restart the executor only. That returns to tree `e5fd255b` with `zzzzzz-s025.conf` and the s025 config (stake 0.25, budget 2.10, `rpc_rps` 4.0). If the trigger was a (c) rate trigger and it persists on `e5fd255b`, Am.10's own rollback (remove the `rpc_rps` key) follows. An Am.6 fills `no` writes the lane STOP file and then goes back to the 0.10 config as in precondition 5. The owner's install word covers these rollbacks (Am.10 (h) pattern).

**Scaling past this step.** A stake above 0.25 SOL or a budget above 4.00 SOL needs a new amendment with the owner's word and a price-impact estimate (§6); a budget above 4.00 also needs a code-ceiling PR.

**Amendment 12 Erratum 1 (2026-11-18, manager24; precondition 3 named the wrong set of exits).** Precondition 3 says "The eight 0.25 positions open at drafting have had their exits sent". The drafting input (`ops/manager24/AM12-PROPOSAL-DRAFT.md`, "after the 0.25 time-stop exits of 11-18 (~00:55Z-04:47Z)") and the HELM-ASK timing assumed all eight time stops fall in that night window. As written, precondition 3 would hold the install about 12.5 hours past the window the amendment was drafted around.

Correction: precondition 3 reads "**The seven 0.25 positions bought between 2026-11-17T00:55Z and 04:26:16Z (orders 5b5c6a162df54ac1, 386a12143151e807, a9eef6bd5c78ad9e, 683ac45831594ab3, bd6222de3daf171e, 74a24133256b9699, 21528bef56edfa2b) have had their exits sent, and no sell failed with a non-transient error.** Their realized total is reported to the owner as it is." Order `2a1e8667d4ff37a0` and every position opened after drafting keep their exits under the running rules: any non-transient sell failure on any of them, before or after the install, is a lane STOP trigger (HANDOFF section 1), as for every exit; it is an Am.12 rollback trigger only in the case Am.12's rollback list already names (a `send_error` attributed to the new code). Nothing else in Amendment 12 changes: no figure, no other precondition, no QP condition (C1, C2), no rollback step.

## Amendment 9 book-list record, 2026-11-18: osb-v2-u2 (U2 v2 open simulation)
In force from its merge. Written 2026-11-18T06:1xZ (`date -u` 06:00:06Z at the start of the work) by a worker for manager24, at main 24f9f81b. Records only; changes no threshold, window, pin, clock or limit of any other book. Source: QP-RULING-1118-u2-v2 (`/data/mal/hunt-1008/ops/QP-RULING-1118-u2-v2.md`, sha256 `d79c319db4d304e37160a6d7f4c6e8cf3236dcd7069f82908941001198876f30`), section 4 item 4 (the T1 records the ruling requires before S2). **No book has cleared the promotion gate. Nothing here says U2, U4, C1-NF or any book makes money.** Paper only; an open simulation, not a gate read.

Under §4 ("Later books ... start under this amendment"), one more open simulation book: **osb-v2-u2**, the second book of the U2 rule (cohort-residual relative strength; rule, constants, latency set, fee and rent modes as in the osb-u2 record of 2026-11-16 above and QP-RULING-1116-u4-u2-p81-forward-specs `297657b433180e8e8dd8233ed30d1d98885e8a3575431f0d9f054b2e26fda764`, its ADD1 `e5009a3c0e3ecb2716befc62ae6fc5b2aa7553fec18a2c9f7f055085f66d6665` and ADD2 `ff5ea10c41e6e24b1c21e5b6c08edd01a7d4c06e5a395013fd8aab6e4fccac44`, and QP-RULING-1117-u4u2-bounded-state `622c4734fdb5d6785e69510391af8c57206b06c4356315f99e6a87c877b0af91`). It fixes osb-u2's pricing-coverage defect (QP-RULING-1118-u2-v2 section 1) and changes nothing else. osb-u2 keeps running unchanged to its L1 (and L2 if n < 10); it is never restarted on the v2 commit.

- **Book.** `osb-v2-u2` (deciding only at S2: entry latency 19 slots = 4.085 s, send 505,000 lamports per send, rent stranded 2,039,280 lamports per fill; stakes 0.10 / 0.25 / 0.5 SOL from the same decisions, 0.5 model only, extrapolated, not for steering). The twins (`osb-v2-u2-lat3010`, `osb-v2-u2-lat5590`) are not started; they start later only by full replay and `--compare` of the per-hour decision md5, as for osb-u2. - **The fix (QP-RULING-1118-u2-v2 section 2).** `engine_warmup_h: 7`: the rows of [S2 - 7 h, S2) go through `engine.on_row` as forward hours do, with no buy, sell or placebo draw (the warm-up guard and the bots' `clock_start_ms` = S2 still refuse any entry or sell before S2). `engine_prune: false`: `run_hour` skips `engine.prune()`; `trim_history()` stays at every hour end. Both keys absent means the old behaviour, which the off-key equivalence proof (test 4b below) covers for the U2-v1 manifest.

W2 = [max(2026-11-03T03:00Z, S2 - 336 h), S2) = [2026-11-04T12:00Z, S2) (336 h, required >= 192 h). Engine warm-up E = [S2 - 7 h, S2) = [2026-11-18T05:00Z, S2), inside W2. Decisions off in code before S2: no universe pass, ranking, breadth, fire, entry, placebo draw, price or outcome is computed for any mark before S2, and the ledger holds zero rows with an instant before S2 (a row that does voids the book).

Read rule unchanged: spec §0.8, GO conditions (1) to (6), the 20% cap on excluded entries (engine refusals count as "other" exclusions). The licence path for U2 is this look only; osb-u2's look is reported beside it and cannot license U2 by itself (QP-RULING-1118-u2-v2 section 1c). Not a gate read, never part of a later read's data.

  6. The v2 engine-state line (ruling section 2): "engine state starts at S2 - 7 h: a pool whose pricing would need a print before then (V fallback) is priced as the engine prices it, or excluded and counted".
  7. The 9 rows of this book count in n_books_watched and the best-of-N sentence. 0.10 and 0.25: "model; no live fills for this rule"; 0.5: "model only, extrapolated; not for steering".
- **Disclosure for osb-u2, required by the ruling (verbatim on every osb-u2 board note, its look, the osb-v2-u2 look and any U2 proposal; brackets filled unrounded from the board).** "osb-u2 (U2 v1) has a simulator defect found from code on 2026-11-18 (QP-RULING-1118-u2-v2): its pricing engine was not fed warm-up hours and dropped pools quiet for more than 20,000 slots (about 2.2 h), while U2 picks pools whose last print can be up to 6 h old. Picks the engine had not seen were refused (no_prints), and at the first mark the slot clock was not ready (clock_not_ready). As of <instant>: <r> of <N> forward entries refused (no_prints <a>, clock_not_ready <b>); placebo <r_p> of <N_p>. Its priced trades are a subset biased towards recently active pools. osb-u2 cannot license U2 by itself; its look is reported beside osb-v2-u2 and never pooled with it."
- **U4 and C1-NF.** No change; neither moves to the v2 commit (QP-RULING-1118-u2-v2 section 3). If either board ever shows a `no_prints` or `clock_not_ready` refusal decided after the first hour past its S, the manager reports it to QP; it is not repaired in place.
- **Memory.** Memory priority goes to osb-v2-u2: if research-0 cannot hold both U2 processes beside U4 and C1-NF, osb-u2 stops at that instant and its complete-day board as of the stop is published as "osb-u2 stopped <instant> for memory: partial, not a look".

Paper only. Nothing here says U2 makes money. No book has cleared the promotion gate.

## Amendment 13 (2026-11-18): floor-lane-1 decay monitor step-down binding (QP-RULING-1118-floor-brake, condition C1) (DRAFT)

**Status: DRAFT until merge. Binding in full only from the merge of the per-close monitor code PR (item 5); before that, hourly-state only (declared limitation, item 5).** Its merge needs QP OK and REVIEWER APPROVE at the exact head. At merge the step-down binds as a **manager duty to file the HELM-ASK the same hour**; the install itself still waits for the owner's word to Helm at that time until the owner's standing word is quoted in a T1 erratum to this amendment (item 1 (d)). Until then the step-down is, in effect, the ruling's advisory state plus a mandatory ask; the STOP row (item 2) is binding at merge. Written 2026-11-18T18:40Z (`date -u`) by a records worker for manager24, at main bb0322ba. Records only: no code, config, host, unit or live file is touched. No book has cleared the promotion gate, and nothing here says FLOOR makes money. No gate threshold, seal or clock changes.

**Source.** `/data/mal/hunt-1008/ops/QP-RULING-1118-floor-brake.md` (sha256 `4f9333c322fce3ced980c0165e0038117b2c0775f6f969319b31b12ea0c6418b`), section A (the monitor) and section C item C1: "A DEC-035 amendment (T1 records PR: separate QP OK and REVIEWER APPROVE at the exact head, merged) that quotes section A, names the 0.10 rollback target, and states that the step-down and its HELM-ASK are covered by the owner's install word (Am.10 (h) / Am.12 rollback pattern) or quotes the owner's word for it." Until this merges the step-down row is advisory (ruling C1); the STOP row is always available.

**The owner's direction** (MiScusi chat, 2026-11-18 about 06Z; verbatim as the manager's memory note `owner-raise-bar-1118` and the ruling's header record it, the note's own ellipses kept): "Is there any way we can watch out for bad market hours, stop ourselves from wiping out our profits? I don't want to inhibit any profit whatsoever". This is a direction, not the owner's word for this amendment's step-down (see item 1 (d)).

**The monitor (ruling section A, as merged in `tools/floor_decay_monitor.py`; restated, not changed; the ruling text controls).** Live floor-lane-1 closed trades only; r = realized SOL after all fees, nothing dropped; s = the stake of the latest ledger `start` row; the population is the trailing run of start rows at that stake (a stake change starts a new population; 0.10 and 0.25 trades are never mixed). N = 20 closed trades. **X1** last-20 mean <= -0.05 s; **X2** population realized drawdown from its high-water mark >= 3 s; **Y1** last-20 mean <= -0.15 s; **Y2** lane lifetime realized drawdown from its lifetime high-water mark >= 1.50 SOL (absolute). Below 20 closed trades only X2 and Y2 apply. At s = 0.25: X1 -0.0125 SOL per trade, X2 0.75 SOL, Y1 -0.0375 SOL per trade. Exit codes: **10** step-down (X1 or X2 at a stake above 0.10); **20** STOP (Y1, Y2, or X1/X2 at 0.10, ruling A4 row 3); **2** input error; 0 no flag. The ruling's power note stands: this is a brake, not a test, and it cannot say FLOOR has an edge.

**What this amendment binds (manager duties).**

1. **Exit 10 = step-down to 0.10.**
   - (a) In the same hour as the run that exits 10, the manager files one HELM-ASK: executor-only restart (never the host) onto the **pre-staged step-down config** `/etc/mal-floor-lane/live-5fc473b5256cdd1d0612fec6d460cb4a19d5bf1a-s010-b400.json`, on the running tree `/usr/local/lib/mal-floor-lane-executor/5fc473b5256cdd1d0612fec6d460cb4a19d5bf1a`, by a new drop-in `zzzzzzzz-s010.conf` that sorts after `zzzzzzz-b400.conf`. The config is the running Am.12 config `live-5fc473b5256cdd1d0612fec6d460cb4a19d5bf1a-s025-b400.json` (sha256 `ff21f89286eb10af3a899a902c09b918516e76128968b214cb5809db1194bb97`, HELM-REPLY-1118-1) with exactly one value changed: `stake_lamports` 250000000 to **100000000**. Budget 4.00, daily stop 0.50, `max_open` 16, reserve, wallet floor, `rpc_rps` 4.0 and the absent `presend_batch` key are unchanged. The start line after the restart must end exactly `stake=100000000 rpc_rps=4.0 presend_batch=false`. **Any other tail is a failed install:** the manager writes the lane STOP file at once (item 2 command), because the X flag that caused the step-down still holds and ruling A4 row 2 does not allow 0.25 under it; then one HELM-ASK: Helm removes `zzzzzzzz-s010.conf`, runs `systemctl daemon-reload` and restarts the executor only (back to the b400 config, start line `stake=250000000 rpc_rps=4.0 presend_batch=false`). The lane stays STOPPED until the 0.10 config is installed and its start line checked, or the owner says otherwise (quoted in a record).
   - (b) **Pre-staging.** Within a day of this merge the manager files one HELM-ASK asking Helm to write that config and drop-in file (not to enable or restart anything) and to report the config's sha256 and its diff against the b400 config (one key). The sha256 goes in the state file and in each step-down ask. If a later install moves the executor tree or config, that install's ask names the matching 0.10 step-down config (same rule: the running config with only `stake_lamports` 100000000) and its sha256; the step-down target follows the running config, never an older tree. (Ruling A4 named the target of its day, tree `e5fd255b` with `zzzzz-pin-e5fd.conf`, config sha256 `32991fe5...`, and said to re-check it at the time; Am.12 has since moved the tree to `5fc473b5`, so that target would also change the code and the budget and is not used.)
   - (c) The ask and its install wait for no install inside the 15 minutes before a time stop (ruling A4). Open positions and lane cash are recorded at the ask (an executor restart empties the marks, Am.4 item 6). Open 0.25 positions keep their exits.
   - (d) **Who restarts: Helm, not the manager.** HANDOFF section 2 item 1: "The executor `mal-floor-lane.service` is also restarted only through Helm." The owner's 2026-11-13 restart pre-approval covers "future FLOOR restarts on the same code and config (startup-only fixes; excludes new code, caps, keys, funds, the clock)" (HELM-REPLY-1113-2, as quoted in Amendment 8 "Install"). A stake change is a config change, so that approval **does not cover** the step-down. The Am.12 install word ("Yes to HELM-ASK-1118-1: 4.00 SOL budget, lifetime loss bound about 4.15 SOL, up to about 4.31 SOL in one day", HELM-REPLY-1118-1-owner-yes) names the budget and its rollback to s025, not a stake of 0.10, so it does not cover it either. Therefore the pre-staging ask of (b) asks for **the owner's own word in the Helm chat, once, that Helm installs the pre-staged 0.10 config on any monitor exit 10 with no further owner word** (Am.10 item 6 (h) pattern), given on the 0.10 risk figures of (h). **That word is not yet given.** Owner word requested 2026-11-18 (manager24 session); until it is recorded verbatim in an erratum or Helm reply, each step-down install needs the owner's word to Helm at that time; the manager files the HELM-ASK the same hour and writes nothing else. The standing word counts as pre-authorising the install only from the merge of a T1 erratum to this amendment that quotes it verbatim (as Am.12 quoted its word), not from a state-file or notebook line alone.
   - (e) Records, the same hour: a state-file line, a MiScusi notebook entry (kind decision, the monitor output lines `flags:` / `FIRED` / `population start`, no price, mint or signature), and a LAB_STATE "Now" row. The owner is told in the next owner-visible update. When Helm reports the install, the restart is recorded in LAB_STATE (CLAUDE.md mid-week restart rule) with the start line as printed.
   - (f) Until Helm installs it, nothing else changes: no manual sell, no config or pin change, no other restart. If the monitor exits 20 while the step-down waits, item 2 applies at once.
   - (g) The step-down restart is a config change: it ends any open Am.10 item 5 window, which is read on the live buys so far and marked "n < 10" (Am.11 install precedent). The rule "no second install is made inside a window" does not hold back a step-down. Other checks in force (the Am.6 fills check, the recon) run as written.
   - (h) **Risk at 0.10 (QP arithmetic on PR #1452 at 8858bd21; structural bounds, not a forecast).** No loss bound rises. Budget slots become floor(4.00 / 0.10386) = 38, so `max_open` 16 binds (not the budget's 15 at 0.25): at most 16 x 0.10386 = 1.66176 SOL of stake plus reserve out at once, against 15 x 0.25386 = 3.81 SOL at 0.25. One UTC day can lose at most min(remaining room, 0.50 + 16 x 0.10386 = **2.16176 SOL**), against min(remaining room, 4.31) at 0.25, since the daily stop is checked before an entry only. The lifetime bound does **not** fall: it stays budget 4.00 plus lifetime realized (about **4.15 SOL** at the Am.12 ask); a step-down slows losses, it does not lower the cap. The effective lifetime brake is Y2 at 1.50 SOL of drawdown.

2. **Exit 20 = lane STOP file at once.** The manager creates `/var/lib/mal-live/h5-lane/STOP` with one mal-fast-0 job, as HANDOFF section 1 gives it: `sudo -n -u mal-live touch /var/lib/mal-live/h5-lane/STOP && sudo -n ls -la /var/lib/mal-live/h5-lane/STOP`. It stops new entries only; open positions keep their exits (no manual sell). Then, the same hour: owner told (money at risk), notebook entry, state-file line, LAB_STATE row, report to QP. This needs no Helm step (DEC-035 §2 item 6: any session may create it). Removal: only by the manager, after a QP ruling and a notebook note; resumption only at stake 0.10 (the item 1 config installed first if the lane was at 0.25) and only with the owner's word quoted in a record (ruling A4 row 1).

3. **Exit 2, or a stake basis other than the latest ledger `start` row = alert, no action.** The monitor prints `stake_basis`; any value other than "latest ledger start row" means the population is not anchored to a start line. The manager records the alert (state line and notebook) and fixes the input; it is not a step-down or a STOP. While it lasts the manager may compute section A by hand from the lane report (ruling C1), and the STOP file of item 2 stays available.

4. **Back up from 0.10 to 0.25.** Never automatic. All of: (a) at least 20 closed live trades at 0.10 since the step-down start line (the monitor's 0.10 population), with realized >= 0 over them (borrowed from the DEC-036 §6 item 5 up-step rule as a stricter condition) **and** a last-20 mean >= +0.05 s (ruling A4 "Re-arm"); (b) no X or Y flag at the run that reads it, and no flag FIRED in the 0.10 population; (c) a T1 step record with QP OK at its exact head; (d) the owner's word quoted in that record (DEC-035 §6 and ruling A4: a stake increase needs the owner's word, as in Amendments 6 and 11). Then one HELM-ASK: remove `zzzzzzzz-s010.conf`, daemon-reload, executor-only restart; the start line must end `stake=250000000 rpc_rps=4.0 presend_batch=false`. This is a new 0.25 population (the monitor restarts its window). The +0.05 s test is a sizing condition only: ruling A3, "It is a brake, not a test. It does not, and cannot, say FLOOR has an edge".

5. **Cadence.** The monitor runs once an hour as the first step of the manager's hourly job (`/data/mal/hunt-1008/ops/manager24/hourly-submit.sh`, already true; it prints `decay-exit=`), and the hourly job's successor keeps it first. **The ladder is evaluated at every close** (ruling A4: "evaluated at each close and at each hourly print"; its A3 false-alarm figures assume windows checked at every close). At each hourly run the monitor evaluates the flags at each close since the previous run, from the ledger rows, and a flag that fired at any such close counts as fired even if it no longer holds at the run: Y1, Y2, or X at 0.10 means item 2 at once; X at 0.25 means item 1. A missed or failed hour is not a trigger by itself; the next run evaluates every close since the last successful run. **This needs a monitor code change:** a `--since-ms` per-close evaluation in `tools/floor_decay_monitor.py`, a separate T1 code PR (separate QP and reviewer) that the manager is commissioning. **Declared limitation until that PR merges: hourly-state only.** The merged monitor's exit code is the state at the run, and its `fired_ms` holds only the FIRST close at which each flag ever held, so a flag that fires and clears between runs is not seen when it has fired before. In that interval the manager acts on the exit code of each run and, where a flag's printed `FIRED` close is after the previous run's instant, acts on it per ruling A4 as if the run had exited with it.

6. **Unchanged.** No code change in this PR (the executor stays as merged; the monitor changes only by the item 5 code PR). No change to the budget (4.00), daily stop (0.50), `max_open` (16), wallet floor, A3 pause, any seal, any recon band or the promotion gate. The monitor's constants (N, X1, X2, Y1, Y2) are the ruling's and are not edited here. The ruling's bad-hour overlays O1 to O4 (section B) stay a paper study with its own adoption rule (E2 read at or after 2026-11-27T02:00:00Z); none is adopted here. FLOOR keeps its own monitor and constants: DEC-036 §6 (CUSUM decay for rotation books) does not apply to floor-lane-1, and this amendment does not apply to rotation books.

**Why the step-down does not inhibit profit more than it must.** It keeps the lane trading at 0.10, so fills and learning continue; only size falls. The ruling's a-priori false-alarm figures (assumed σ, unmeasured): X1 about 2% per window at a true mean of +0.08 s, about 8% at +0.04 s, and roughly 25 to 35% over 100 trades at +0.04 s. A false alarm costs reduced size until item 4, not lost trades.

- **What P1-P3 do at this pin.**
  - P1, window: a row counts when its `decision_ms.entry` is in [S, L); every count, G1 included, is taken on the windowed set. Buy-side drops (excluded and refused rows) are placed by the entry decision interval, with earliest decision = t_ms - (HOLD_MS + TICK_MS) = 86,402,900 ms (`arena_rule_l1_oq.HOLD_MS` + `arena_rules.TICK_MS`, imported); rows that cannot be placed certainly in or out are ambiguous and are counted in, and `share_without_ambiguous` is printed beside the share. A row with no resolvable time refuses the read.
  - P2, conversion costs: the totals are labelled PARTIAL (conversion legs plus sends; they exclude the token legs' pool fees and impact, which the rows do not carry). The shrink cost follows the QP M5 ruling: c_used = max(c_registered, max(all-rows, priced-only mean (conv + sends)/stake) + token parts), with c_registered 0.07925 and token parts 0.024 + 0.01105 at stake 0.10, and c_registered 0.08348 and token parts 0.024 + 0.0274 at stake 0.25 (these match PR #1458 R2).
  - P3, addresses: addresses appear only in the 0600 `--out` file in a 0700 dir; stdout is always the stripped form.
- **Disclosures (reviewer notes on #1465).** (a) In the rare case of a feed gap that ends after a decision's hold bound, a feed_gap_entry exclusion on the first print after the gap can land later than t_dec + HOLD_MS; that case is not separately stated by the scorer. The late row is placed certainly outside the window, so the effect can only understate the board excluded share, and only by buy-side drops of decisions just before L. (b) The `g1` share denominator is the board denominator and includes the ambiguous buy-side positions, so it is slightly lower than the share without them.
- **Use.** Interim figures from `tools/arena_oq_score.py` may be read only after this erratum merges (QP r3), run by the manager at this pin. The 2026-11-18 read rule (card §11), looks, criteria and shrink prior are unchanged. Method card v4 ([docs/rotation/books/osb-oq-v1.md](../docs/rotation/books/osb-oq-v1.md) §2c, §11, §12, §14). - **What this pin does not do.** (1) The board's own best-of-N sentence counts only its BOOKS.json rows; the board has no `--n-watched` option. (2) The board's "forward disclosure (QP floor)" block is printed for stake 0.25 only and is FLOOR's Am.9 §3 floor, not osb-old's card §4 criterion; it is not cited for osb-old. Numbers are quoted unrounded from the board; a negative total is written as negative. The first line of every interim figure, verbatim: "Interim, complete days to <date>, before the look instant L1 = 2026-12-08T04:00:00Z: not a read, not a GO or NO-GO, and not evidence of an edge." No action on interim figures. The card §4 bars (at least 14 full-coverage days and 30 closed positions) still bind for the first read. Pooling: never.

Paper only. Nothing here says osb-old makes money. No book has cleared the promotion gate.

## Amendment 14 (2026-11-21): floor-lane-1 stake step 0.25 to 0.40 SOL, budget 4.00 to 5.00 SOL, daily stop 0.50 to 0.80 SOL (owner override; DRAFT)

**Status: DRAFT until merge. NOT installed. It authorises nothing until all of these hold: QP OK and REVIEWER APPROVE at the exact head; the merge; the ceiling code PR #1476 merged; the Am.14 ask record (precondition 2) merged; every precondition below met and recorded; the owner's word on the risk table as recomputed at the ask (precondition 2) and the owner's own install word in the Helm chat (precondition 3), both still OPEN; and a Helm install.** Drafted 2026-11-21 (`date -u` about 05:3xZ; revised about 05:4xZ for QP-RULING-1121a and the QP and reviewer findings at f3253640; the commit times are the record) by a records worker for manager24, at main 095d82b5 (DEC-035 file sha256 before this amendment `cf5cc9a5ad21d7be448759f6c7a8e771dc7d735635b33183e3a22bc3bc926702`). Records only: no code, config, host, unit or live file is touched. **floor-lane-1 has not cleared the promotion gate, and nothing here says FLOOR makes money.** No gate threshold, seal or clock changes.

**Sources.**
- `/data/mal/hunt-1008/ops/QP-RULING-1121-floor-stake-step.md` (sha256 `3e51d68e8c48754dbbb1816e99f939a8a22d0ae7d4da270e4297d5a0a8c56c67`; quant-proof, `date -u` 2026-11-21T05:19:44Z, at origin/main 095d82b5): §1.2 items 1 to 14, §2 (impact), §3 (model value), §4 (sequencing) and §5 (rollback triggers).

It also corrects the parent's no-top-up wallet-floor count (§6 erratum).

This amendment restates the two rulings. On any conflict the ruling text controls, with 1121a ahead of 1121 where it supersedes it.

Ruling 1121 §1.1: "A PASS at 0.25 is necessary" (QP-RULING-1120 §1.2 item 11) is a condition of the gate route, not DEC-035 text. An owner override in the Am.4/6/11/12 pattern needs no PASS. It needs a new amendment with the stake, a price-impact estimate at that stake and the owner's word (DEC-035 Am.6 "A stake above 0.25 SOL ... requires the owner's word in a new amendment", Am.11 "Unchanged", Am.12 "Scaling past this step").

**Decider.** Vaan (owner).

**The owner's word on the step (MiScusi session chat, at or before 2026-11-21T05:28:36Z, the stamp on the manager's state-file line that records it verbatim).** The manager's update before it showed:
- the modelled SOL/day table, at stakes 0.25, 0.40 and 0.50 under the LIVE-FIT, NOV-CENTRAL and NOV-LOW scenarios of ruling 1121 §3;
- the step-back rule (ruling 1121 §5).

The owner answered, verbatim:

> "I think it's a good idea, if we can get 5 more dollars per day that seems worth it. I'm trying to get the 2 sol tomorrow, it's just taking a while, so that's on me and I'll get it asap, we just have to do with what we have for another day or two. And after this bump, the next 10-12 bucks we should be working towards as much as possible."

The manager reads this in four parts.
- **The step.** It is the owner's word on the step to 0.40 SOL. The "5 more dollars per day" is the LIVE-FIT upside in ruling 1121 §3.4, about +0.07 SOL/day at 0.40 and budget 5.00. That figure is a model fitted to 4 days of the running book, picked after a good run. It is not evidence and not a forecast.
- **The top-up.** The owner's 2.0 SOL top-up has **not** landed. The owner is "trying to get the 2 sol tomorrow" and says the lane will "do with what we have for another day or two".
- **The last sentence.** "the next 10-12 bucks we should be working towards" is a direction on later targets. It is not a word for any step above 0.40. Item 14 still applies, and ruling 1121 §3.4 says 0.50 is not worth it before 12-07.
- **What it does not cover.** It is **not** yet the owner's word on this amendment's risk table as recomputed at the ask (ruling 1121 §1.2 item 1; QP C1). It is not the install word in the Helm chat either (Am.10 item 6 (d)). Both are open preconditions (2 and 3 below).

**Scope of the override.** It overrides §6 ("only through the promotion gate") for this step and for floor-lane-1 only, as Amendments 4 to 6, 11 and 12 did. It does not lower the promotion gate (CLAUDE.md): **floor-lane-1 has not cleared the gate**, and nothing in this amendment says it makes money. An override step is not a promotion (ruling 1121 §1.1).

**What this amendment changes, and only this (ruling 1121 §1.2 items 2 to 6).**
1. **Stake.** `stake_lamports` 250,000,000 to **400000000** (one value).
2. **Budget.** `budget_lamports` 4,000,000,000 to **5,000,000,000** (5.00 SOL). It is the Am.3 full-wipe bound and the `committed_loss_stop` budget.
   - At 0.40 the 4.00 budget admits floor(4.00 / 0.40386) = 9 opens, against 15 at 0.25. That turns a signal-limited book into a capital-refused one (ruling 1121 §1.2 item 4, §3.3).
   - At 5.00 it admits floor(5.00 / 0.40386) = 12, with lifetime realized >= 0 and no 0.25 position open.
   - The step is not installed at budget 4.00 (ruling 1121: "Do not install a step at budget 4.00").
   - **The budget is never lowered back to 4.00 by a config swap in this amendment** (ruling 1121a §2; rollback below).
3. **Daily stop.** `daily_loss_lamports` 500,000,000 to **800,000,000** (0.80 SOL = 0.50 x 0.40 / 0.25; ruling 1121 §1.2 item 5, scaled). It stays marked P&L, checked before an entry only.
   - With 12 open at 0.40 (4.84632 SOL of stake plus reserve out), 0.80 SOL is a marked fall of about 16.5%.
   - The drop at the fill uses part of that room on every new entry. That drop is the cost stack plus the 2.22% model fill premium (price impact below).
   - Kept at 0.50, it would have been about 10.3%, and the stop would trip more often and cut trades (ruling 1121 item 5).
4. **Code ceilings: separate T1 code PR #1476 (`claude/floor-ceiling-040`), merged before the install** (ruling 1121 §1.2 item 3). At tree `5fc473b5`, in `tools/floor_lane_executor.py`:
   - `FLOOR_LANE_STAKE_BOUNDS_LAMPORTS = (20_000_000, 250_000_000)` (:50): upper bound to 400,000,000, with the test that pins it;
   - `LANE_BUDGET_MAX_LAMPORTS = 4_000_000_000` (:58): to 5,000,000,000;
   - `FLOOR_LANE_DAILY_MAX_LAMPORTS = 500_000_000` (:61): to 800,000,000.

   PR #1476 itself changes nothing else (+5/-5 on `tools/floor_lane_executor.py`, ceilings only). **The tree move brings more code than the ceilings** (reviewer B2 at 60b0664c). Main since `5fc473b5` already carries the l1-floor-v3 rule plumbing, and the install moves the executor onto it:
   - 63c8b270 "floor_lane_host: l1-floor-v3 rule pin plumbing (config rule_id + q_star_lamports); tests";
   - fab48eaf "floor host v3: record q_star in entry meta + start-row binding, refuse resume at another q_star; lazy v3 import; v2 null-key refusal; cooldown fixture pin";
   - 08854792 "floor arena executor: rule_id config (l1-floor-v2 default, l1-floor-v3 pinned q_star); v2 unchanged";
   - e44a6047 "floor lane executor: pass rule_id arm and expected q_star through; v2 lane start row unchanged".

   `git diff --numstat 5fc473b5 095d82b5` over the Am.12 item 2 executor file list shows `tools/floor_arena_executor.py` +55/-9 (`rule_id` config, `RULE_V3`, `Q_STAR_ALLOWED`, `expected_q_star`, a `q_star_pin` refusal and a `q_star_mismatch` latch) and `tools/floor_lane_executor.py` +4/-4 (`expected_q_star` passthrough, start row `book=cfg.bot_id`). With #1476 (head 452f296c) the same list shows `tools/floor_arena_executor.py` +55/-9 and `tools/floor_lane_executor.py` +9/-9 (ceilings +5/-5 plus the v3 passthrough +4/-4). `tools/floor_lane_host.py` also changed (+61/-11); it is the decision host, which this install does not restart.

   **No Am.14 config carries a `rule_id` or an `expected_q_star_lamports` key.** That holds for s040-b500, R1, R2 and S10, as it does for the running s025-b400. So the arm is the default `l1-floor-v2`, the v3 paths stay inactive, and v2 refuses a stray `expected_q_star_lamports` key at load (`tools/floor_arena_executor.py:92-95` at 095d82b5). The code's own word that v2 is unchanged is a comment, not a proof. The proof that v2 decisions are unchanged across both deltas is the md5 replay of precondition 1.
5. **Executor tree.** It is pinned to the merge commit of PR #1476: `<AT-ASK: new tree sha (full 40 hex), and the blob shas of each changed executor file>`. The check at the ask passes only on both of these:

   - `git diff --numstat 5fc473b5..<new tree>` over the Am.12 item 2 executor file list showing exactly the changes of the four commits named in item 4 plus #1476, and nothing else. The expected figures are those above, `tools/floor_arena_executor.py` +55/-9 and `tools/floor_lane_executor.py` +9/-9: `<AT-ASK: numstat at the merge commit>`.

   A tree at any other commit is a new ask (Am.10 (g) pattern). This is new live code under Am.10 (g) and item 6 (d), so the owner's Helm-chat install word (precondition 3) covers it.

   It is called **s040-b500** below. Path: `/etc/mal-floor-lane/live-<new tree>-s040-b500.json`. sha256: `<AT-ASK: sha256 of the new config, and Helm's diff against the s025-b400 config showing those three lines only>`. Drop-in: `<AT-ASK: drop-in name, sorting after every drop-in present, e.g. zzzzzzzzz-s040-b500.conf>`.

**Unchanged.**
- `max_open` 16 (it does not bind below 12 slots).
- The 0.75 SOL wallet floor, held once.
- Reserve 3,860,000 lamports per position.
- `rpc_rps` 4.0, with `presend_batch` absent.

- The seals, the clocks and the promotion gate.

Open 0.25 positions keep their 0.25 stakes and their exits.

**Wallet floor and slots (ruling 1121 §1.2 item 6; computed at the ask with the code's formula, `tools/floor_arena_executor.py:184-193` at `5fc473b5`).**
- need = unlanded held stakes + 400,000,000 + 160,000 + 2,100,000 + (n + 1) x 1,600,000 + 750,000,000, checked against the free balance (`getBalance`) before each send.
- `committed_loss_stop` refuses when max(0, -realized) + Σ (worst case of each open order at its own stake) + 403,860,000 > 5,000,000,000. The worst case is 253,860,000 for a 0.25 order and 403,860,000 for a 0.40 order.
- The Am.3 full-wipe pre-send arm is computed the same way at the ask.
- Open 0.25 positions keep their 0.25 stakes in both sums until they close, so for about 24 h after the install the slot count is mixed. With k open at 0.25, budget slots for new 0.40 opens = floor((5,000,000,000 - max(0, -realized) - k x 253,860,000) / 403,860,000).
- **Drafting estimate.** QP arithmetic on the brief's figures, not recomputed here. Wallet at cost is about free 1.46 + 10 x 0.25226 = 3.98 SOL, an assumption about n. Wallet-floor slots at 0.40:
  - **7** with no top-up (QP-RULING-1121a §6 erratum, correcting the parent ruling's 8: each open takes about 0.40220 SOL, and the 8th open falls short by about 0.0005 SOL before priority fees and tips);
  - **10** with +1.0 SOL;
  - **12** with +2.0 SOL.

  Budget slots at 5.00: 12 (all at 0.40, realized >= 0).
- At the ask:
  - n open = `<AT-ASK: n>`, of which at 0.25 = `<AT-ASK: k>`;
  - free balance = `<AT-ASK: lamports>`;
  - lifetime realized = `<AT-ASK: SOL, from the lane report lifetime_realized_sol>`;
  - top-up landed = `<AT-ASK: yes/no and amount>`;
  - wallet-floor slot table = `<AT-ASK: slots at +0 / +1.0 / +2.0 SOL from the free balance and n by the need formula above, each further open checked at its own n>`;
  - budget slots = `<AT-ASK: by the mixed formula above>`.

  **The ask states the lower of the budget count and the wallet-floor count.**
- **Top-up.** The owner's top-up should land **before** the install, or the step thins the trade rate. Ruling 1121 item 6 says so, and §3.3 models 0.40 at budget 5.00 with +1 SOL at about 0.89 to 0.93 of today's throughput.
  - The owner is "trying to get the 2 sol tomorrow". The manager installs after it lands.
  - An earlier install needs the ask to state the no-top-up slot count (drafting estimate 7, against 12 now at 0.25, ruling 1121 §3.3 row 1). The owner's Helm-chat word must then name that count.
  - A top-up that lands after the install takes effect with no restart (Am.12 item 4). The manager then records the balance, n and the recomputed count the same hour.

**Price impact (§6; ruling 1121 §2 option (A), the required minimum; model, not fills).** Flat-pool constant product, V = 18 SOL (Am.6 "Price impact"):

| Stake | Post-buy price / floor ((18+q)/18)² | Average fill premium q/18 | Round-trip impact 2q²/(18+q) | As % of stake |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| 0.25 | 1.02797 | 1.389% | 0.006849 SOL | 2.74% |
| 0.40 | 1.04494 | 2.222% | **0.017391 SOL** | 4.35% |

- Impact per trade grows with the stake squared. The assumed gross edge grows only linearly.
- The formula does not capture pools shallower than 18, sandwich or MEV at size, or sells where our size is a large share of the market's flow. The sign of that error is toward optimism (ruling 1121 §2).
- Option (B), live-quote depth from `tools/floor_fills_vs_model.py`, is a recommended supplement. Only the aggregates that ruling 1121 §2 (B) lists may be read: `<AT-ASK (optional, if the (B) job has run): n_closed, n_matched, n with derived Qe, median / p10 / p90 Qe, median_entry_slip_pp, mean_entry_slip_pp, verdict; and impact(0.40) = 2 x 0.16 / (Qe + 0.40) at the median and p10 Qe, labelled model, not fills>`.
- The real measurement is the B1 fills check at the 10th closed 0.40 trade (rollback triggers below). That tool prices the entry model at the live stake.

**Model value (ruling 1121 §3; MODEL ONLY: no fills, no read, no forecast).** net(x) = g·x - 2x²/(18 + x) - 0.00032, with g assumed constant in stake.
- **Per day at 8 trades/day, throughput unconstrained (ruling 1121 §3.2):**
  - LIVE-FIT: 0.205 SOL/day at 0.25, against 0.278 at 0.40. It rests on 4 days of the running book, picked after a good run; it is not out-of-sample.
  - NOV-CENTRAL (in-sample): 0.056 against 0.039.
  - NOV-LOW (stress, ex-top-3): -0.053 against -0.135.
- **With slots (ruling 1121 §3.3; Erlang-B, offered load an assumption):** 0.40 at budget 5.00 with +2 SOL keeps today's throughput (LIVE-FIT 0.278 SOL/day). With +1 SOL it is 0.248 to 0.258.
- **Break-even (model):** the step beats 0.25 only if the true net per trade at 0.25 is above +0.0104 SOL. Equivalently, the realized mean per trade at 0.40 must exceed **+0.0104 SOL**.
- **Ruling 1121 §3.4:**
  - upside about +0.07 SOL/day (about +$5/day) if the live edge is real;
  - downside, against staying at 0.25, about -0.017 SOL/day if NOV-CENTRAL is the truth, or about -0.08 SOL/day if NOV-LOW is;
  - the rollback caps the downside to roughly one probation, about 30 trades (under NOV-LOW, about 0.30 SOL of extra loss).
- The owner's $30/day (about 0.44 SOL/day) is not reachable through FLOOR's stake; the model optimum is about 0.32 SOL/day. The gap has to come from more signals or stacked books.

**Risk in plain words (ruling 1121 §1.2 item 7).** The owner's word must be given on these figures as recomputed at the ask (precondition 2). Drafting figures use the parent ruling's brief: lifetime realized about +0.834 SOL, and wallet at cost about 3.98 SOL with n assumed 10.

| | 0.40, budget 5.00, daily 0.80 (this amendment) | 0.25, budget 4.00, daily 0.50 (running, Am.12) |
| --- | --- | --- |
| Most the lane can still lose (budget + lifetime realized) | about **5.83 SOL** (5.00 + 0.834). At the ask: `<AT-ASK: 5.00 + lifetime realized>`. It stays 5.00 + realized after any rollback (R1, R2, S10 below). | about 4.83 SOL |
| Most at stake at once | 12 x 0.40386 = **4.85 SOL** with +2.0 SOL; 7 x 0.40386 = **2.83 SOL** with no top-up (QP-RULING-1121a §6). At the ask: `<AT-ASK: slots stated x 0.40386, plus k x 0.25386 for open 0.25 positions while they last>` | 15 x 0.25386 = 3.81 SOL (budget), lower where the wallet floor binds |
| What one UTC day can lose | min(remaining room, 0.80 + 12 x 0.40386 = **5.65 SOL**): nearly all of it, since the daily stop is checked before an entry only. At the ask: `<AT-ASK: min(remaining room, 0.80 + slots stated x 0.40386)>` | min(remaining room, 0.50 + 15 x 0.2539 = 4.31) |
| Share of the wallet that can be lost | With +2.0 SOL: wallet at cost about 5.98 SOL and lifetime bound 5.834, so **97.6%**; with the 0.75 SOL floor held, about 5.23 SOL (87.5%) can actually be lost. With no top-up: about 3.23 of 3.98 SOL (81.2%, floor-held). At the ask: `<AT-ASK: lifetime bound / wallet at cost, and (wallet at cost - 0.75) / wallet at cost, with wallet at cost = free balance + open basis (+ top-up if landed)>` | about 78% at the Am.12 ask |
| One trade's full loss | 0.404 SOL | 0.254 SOL |
| Daily P&L swings | about 1.6x larger at the same trade mix | 1x |
| Am.13 Y2 (lifetime drawdown 1.50 SOL, unchanged) | about 3.7 full-loss positions (1.50 / 0.40386), so a STOP is more likely | about 5.9 (1.50 / 0.25386) |

- The positions are in distinct pools, but they are not independent. A program redeploy, an A3 burst or a market-wide dip can hit all open positions at once.
- After a step-back the risk stays inside this table (ruling 1121a §2 and §4; rollback below):
  - **R1:** at most 16 x 0.25386 = 4.06 SOL at stake; one-day bound min(room, 0.80 + 4.06 = 4.86).
  - **R2:** one-day bound min(room, 0.50 + 4.06 = 4.56).
  - **S10:** at most 16 x 0.10386 = 1.66 SOL at stake; one-day bound min(room, 0.50 + 1.66 = 2.16).
  - The lifetime bound stays **5.00 + realized** at R1, R2 and S10. A step-back slows losses; it does not lower the cap (Am.13 (h) wording).
- Evidence is thin:
  - the LIVE-FIT edge is 4 days chosen after a good run;
  - the November in-sample all-entries ex-top-3 is negative at every cap tested (Am.6 Errata 2 and 3);
  - no live fill exists at 0.40.

**Install preconditions (Am.12 pattern; ruling 1121 §1.2 item 9).** All are recorded in the state file before the HELM-ASK. **No install happens while any `<AT-ASK: …>` value in this amendment, other than the optional §2 (B) figures, is unfilled** (reviewer m1). Every filled value is recorded in the Am.14 ask record (precondition 2) and quoted in the HELM-ASK and the install record.

1. **Merges, replay and tests.**
   - This amendment has QP OK and REVIEWER APPROVE at its exact head and is merged.
   - The ceiling PR #1476 is merged with its own exact-head QP OK and REVIEWER APPROVE.
   - **md5 decision-equivalence replay** (CLAUDE.md runner rule): `tools/floor_exec_equiv.py` in its per-row form, as in Am.12 precondition 2. **Post-install check (ruling 1121 §1.2 item 10).**
- The executor start line must end exactly `stake=400000000 rpc_rps=4.0 presend_batch=false`.
- Any other tail is a failed install, and the install-failure rollback below runs at once.
- The 0.40 start-line instant is recorded. It starts the 0.40 population for Am.13 and for B1 to B5.
- The restart is recorded in LAB_STATE (CLAUDE.md mid-week restart rule) with the start line as printed.

**Rollback targets (QP-RULING-1121a §2 to §4; they replace ruling 1121 §1.2 item 11 and its §5 target).**

The rulings give the reason. After an executor restart the marks are empty, so the lane counts every open position at 0. A budget lowered to 4.00 while 0.40 positions are open can then trip `lane_budget_stop`, which writes the lane STOP file (`floor_lane_executor.py:215-216, 522-523` at `5fc473b5`). Even when it does not, `committed_loss_stop` and the full-wipe pre-send refuse every new entry. At the same budget the invariant holds: every entry passed `committed_loss_stop`, so ledger cash > -budget at any later restart (ruling 1121a §1).
- **R1 = s040-b500 with exactly one value changed: `stake_lamports` 400000000 to 250000000.**
  - Budget stays 5,000,000,000, daily stop stays 800,000,000, and every other key is unchanged.
  - Tree: the #1476 merge commit, the Am.14 tree. It is the tree whose decisions the precondition 1 md5 replay shows equal to `5fc473b5` with the s025 config. No other tree may be used.
  - Path `/etc/mal-floor-lane/live-<new tree>-s025-b500.json`, with its own drop-in sorting after the s040-b500 drop-in.
  - A step-back is one HELM-ASK: R1's drop-in is enabled, then daemon-reload and an executor-only restart. R1's start line ends `stake=250000000 rpc_rps=4.0 presend_batch=false`.
- **R2 = R1 with exactly one value changed: `daily_loss_lamports` 800,000,000 to 500,000,000.**
  - Path `/etc/mal-floor-lane/live-<new tree>-s025-b500-d050.json`, with a drop-in sorting after R1's.
  - The manager files the R2 HELM-ASK in the first hourly after a rollback to R1 at which no 0.40 position is open (every 0.40 intent closed or written off in the lane ledger). Never within 15 minutes before a time stop. Executor-only restart.
  - 0.80 is kept while 0.40 positions are open because they carry 0.40-sized marked swings; 0.50 is the owner's daily stop at 0.25 (Am.12). A daily-stop trip only refuses entries and never writes the STOP file.
- **S10 = R2 with exactly one value changed: `stake_lamports` to 100000000.**
  - Budget 5.00 and daily stop 0.50, on the new tree. Path `/etc/mal-floor-lane/live-<new tree>-s010-b500.json`.
  - It is the one 0.10 target in every case after the Am.14 install (item 12 (b) and (d); "Disclosures" (iii)).
- **The budget stays 5.00 after any rollback.** It is never lowered by a config swap in this amendment. Going back to budget 4.00 needs its own T1 record, with QP, a rollback-time ledger-cash check (cash > -4.00 + 0.10 SOL with marks empty) and the owner's word.
- **Install-failure exception.** Suppose the Am.14 install fails its start-line check and the lane ledger shows **no** `send_intent` at 400000000 since the start line. Then the target may be the old s025-b400 on `5fc473b5`: remove the new drop-in, daemon-reload, executor-only restart, and the start line ends `stake=250000000 rpc_rps=4.0 presend_batch=false`. Once any 0.40 intent exists, only R1 is used. An owner stop (the owner's word, quoted in a record, to undo the step) is handled the same way: s025-b400 if no 0.40 intent exists, R1 otherwise. QP accepted this as "safe by the 1121a §1 invariant (with no 0.40 intent, every open position passed `committed_loss_stop` at budget 4.00)" (QP re-review, issuecomment-7058139447, N1). The same no-0.40-intent check from the ledger is recorded before that ask.
- **Post-rollback check.**
  - R1's start line cannot be told apart from the old s025-b400 by the start line alone. So Helm's reply gives the active drop-in (`systemctl cat` or equivalent) and **the sha256 of the config file loaded**.
  - These must equal R1's (or R2's, or S10's) recorded sha256, or s025-b400's under the install-failure exception.
  - Any mismatch, or any other start-line tail, is a failed rollback. The manager writes the lane STOP file at once (Am.13 item 1 (a) pattern; the item 2 command) and files one HELM-ASK to fix it.
- **Rollback-time check** (ruling 1121a §3, a guard, not the target). Before every rollback ask, the manager records the empty-mark lane P&L = lifetime realized - Σ cost of open positions, from the lane report or ledger aggregates. If it is <= -4.99 SOL, the manager writes the lane STOP file first, rolls back anyway (exits keep running) and asks QP. That can happen only by fee drift on failed sells.

**Disclosures (QP C3; also stated in the ask record).**
- (i) **After any step-back, the lifetime bound stays 5.00 + realized**, not 4.00 + realized, until this amendment's budget is changed by its own T1 record. While 0.40 positions are open, new 0.25 entries are refused by `committed_loss_stop` or the full-wipe pre-send until enough of them close: at k = 12 with r >= 0, 4.846 + 0.254 = 5.10 > 5.00. These are refusals, not a STOP. They can last up to the open 0.40 positions' time stops, about 24 h.
- (ii) **A step-back (or R2 or S10) whose start line has any other tail, or whose loaded-config sha256 does not match, means the lane STOP file at once**, then one HELM-ASK to fix it.
- (iii) **When S10 is used.** S10 is used for:
  - the Am.13 item 2 resumption at 0.10 after a Y STOP, whether in the 0.40 population or in a 0.25 population on R1/R2;
  - Am.13 exit 10 in a 0.25 population on R1/R2.

  It runs at budget 5.00, so its lifetime bound is 5.00 + realized, not 4.00. Am.13's `s010-b400` on `5fc473b5` is never used after the Am.14 install (ruling 1121a §4). This supersedes QP C3 (iii)'s narrower "Y STOP in the 0.40 population only".

**Rollback triggers (ruling 1121 §5, restated, with R1 as the target per ruling 1121a §5 and the counting sources per QP C2; the ruling text controls).** The hourly job evaluates them from three sources only: the lane daily report keys of ruling 1121 §2 (D), the decay monitor's population lines, and the fills tool's `verdict`. All are counted on the 0.40 population only (EXP-029 §2 trade definition): realized SOL after all fees, nothing dropped.

**B2 and B3 count the monitor's 0.40 population only** (QP C2). They use `n_closed_population` and `realized_population_lamports`, from a run whose `stake_basis` is the latest ledger start row and whose stake is 400000000. A 0.25 intent that closes after the 0.40 start line counts toward Y2 and lifetime only, never toward B2 or B3. If the monitor prints any other stake basis, that is the Am.13 item 3 alert, and B2 and B3 are computed by hand on the same definition.

- **B1, fills: one check.** At the first hourly at or after the 10th closed 0.40 trade, run `tools.floor_fills_vs_model --config /etc/mal-floor-lane/live-<new tree>-s040-b500.json --since <0.40 start-line instant>`. The config is read for paths only, and `--since` filters by send_intent instant, so 0.25 intents are excluded. If it prints `FILLS_MATCH_MODEL no`, step back to R1.
- **B2, early loss: every hourly.** If the 0.40 population realized is <= **-0.80 SOL** (-2 s), step back to R1. This fires before Am.13's X2 (-3 s = 1.20 SOL drawdown). At R1 the restart cannot trip `lane_budget_stop` (ruling 1121a §5).
- **B3, impact break-even: one decision.** At the first hourly at or after the **30th** closed 0.40 trade, step back to R1 if the population realized sum is < **+0.312 SOL**. That is a mean below +0.0104 SOL/trade, the ruling 1121 §3.2 break-even.
  - This is a sizing rule, not a test. It assumes σ of 0.061 SOL/trade at 0.40 (QP-RULING-1120 Q4 σ about 0.038 at 0.25, scaled x1.6).
  - If the true mean is the LIVE-FIT +0.0348, the chance of a needless step-back is about 1.4% (about 7% if σ is 0.09).
  - At the break-even the chance is 50%.
  - If the truth is NOV-CENTRAL +0.0049, the chance that the step wrongly survives is about 31%.
  - If 30 closes have not happened by S' - 24 h, S' moves later. B3 is never skipped.
- **B4, Am.13 flags: every hourly (per-close once the monitor code PR is merged).** X1 or X2 in the 0.40 population means step back to R1. This amendment replaces Am.13's 0.10 rung for this population only. Y1 or Y2 means the lane STOP file, exactly as Am.13 item 2; resumption is at S10 only.
- **B5, capacity: every hourly in the first 48 h.** Suppose `committed_loss_stop` or `wallet_floor` refusals appear in >= 3 hourly prints in any 24 h while the open count is below the slot count stated in the ask. Then the slot arithmetic was wrong: the manager records it and asks QP. It is not a step-back by itself.

Every step-back:
- is one HELM-ASK covered by the owner's install word (precondition 3);
- goes to R1, then to R2 by the R2 rule above;
- starts a new 0.25 population;
- is recorded the same hour (state line, notebook kind decision, LAB_STATE restart row);
- is told to the owner in the next update, with the realized figure as it is.

After S', B1 to B3 are spent, and only B4 applies, as a FAIL end of the read.

**Amendment 13 (decay monitor) interaction (ruling 1121 §1.2 item 12, with ruling 1121a §4).**
- (a) The monitor's population restarts at the 0.40 start row (Am.13: "a stake change starts a new population"). At s = 0.40: X1 **-0.020 SOL/trade**, X2 **1.20 SOL**, Y1 **-0.060 SOL/trade**. Below 20 closed trades only X2 and Y2 apply.
- (b) **Exit 10 in the 0.40 population steps to 0.25 (R1, then R2), not to 0.10.** Am.13 item 1 names 0.10 for the 0.25 population. This amendment's 0.25 target replaces that rung for the 0.40 population only. **Back at 0.25 (R1/R2), Am.13 applies as written, with S10 as its 0.10 rung** (ruling 1121a §4).
- (c) Y2 stays **1.50 SOL absolute** (lifetime drawdown). That is about 3.7 full-loss positions at 0.40, against about 5.9 at 0.25, so a STOP is more likely. It is disclosed here and not widened, because it is the owner's lifetime brake. The reviewer of PR #1476 made the same point; Y2 is not changed.
- (d) Am.13 item 1 (b): "the step-down target follows the running config, never an older tree". After the Am.14 install, the 0.10 step-down target is **S10** (R2 with `stake_lamports` 100000000: budget 5.00, daily 0.50, new tree), not a copy of s040-b500 and not Am.13's `s010-b400` on `5fc473b5`. Its sha256 is in the ask record (precondition 9). At S10, `max_open` 16 binds: at most 1.66 SOL at stake, a one-day bound of min(room, 2.16) and a lifetime bound of 5.00 + realized. The Am.13 (h) risk line is restated with these figures in the ask record.
- (e) **Re-arm.** Back up from 0.25 to 0.40 after any rollback is never automatic, and it goes to s040-b500. It uses the Am.13 item 4 pattern: >= 20 closed at 0.25 since the rollback, realized >= 0 over them, last-20 mean >= +0.05 s, no flag, a T1 step record with QP OK at its exact head, and the owner's word quoted in that record. Re-arm from 0.10 to 0.25 (Am.13 item 4) goes to R2, with the same conditions.

**Interaction with the gate read (ruling 1121 §1.2 item 13 and §4).** No floor-lane-1 registration is open, and none is opened or changed here. The step and its probation (B1 to B3) finish **before** S'.

Sequencing targets (each depends on the gate before it):
1. 11-21 to 11-22: the owner's word on the risk table, and the top-up.
2. 11-22 to 11-23: this amendment and PR #1476 merged; the md5 replay equal; the tests pass; R1, R2 and S10 pre-staged; the 14-day window reset bundled or scheduled; the ask record merged; the HELM-ASK.
3. **Install target 2026-11-23**, outside the 15 minutes before any time stop.
4. B1 fills check at the 10th closed 0.40 trade (about 11-24/25).
5. B3 at the 30th (about 11-26/27 at 8/day).

A CE1 switch goes under its own bot_id (QP-RULING-1120 Q5). It starts at whatever stake FLOOR holds after the probation.

- **S' registration.** It is merged with S' still in the future.
  - **S' = 2026-11-28T00:00:00Z at the earliest**, or the first 00:00Z after the probation decision and any CE1 install, whichever is later.
  - Its one deciding stake is the stake that survived the probation: **400000000**, or 250000000 (R1/R2 at budget 5.00) if a rollback ran. The registration states its budget and daily stop as they are at S'.
  - At 8 trades/day, C' is about 2026-12-11T00:00Z and R' about 2026-12-12T01:00Z. At 6/day, R' is about 12-16.
  - The read lands after 12-07 on either path. What 12-07 gets is the live P&L at the larger stake, not a gate result.
- **No stake step, budget-down or rule change inside [S', R').** Each one is a FAIL end (QP-RULING-1120 §1.2 item 6, Q5). The registration pre-declares the Am.13 step-down and STOP as FAIL ends. The owner is told **before** S' that any further step during the read (for example to 0.50) ends it FAIL.
- The F set of EXP-029 Erratum 1g rule 3 keeps every trade since S = 2026-11-09T06:00:00Z in raw SOL: mixed stakes, no normalisation (QP-RULING-1120 Q2).

**Scaling past this step (ruling 1121 §1.2 item 14).** A stake above 0.40 SOL or a budget above 5.00 SOL needs a new amendment with the owner's word and a price-impact estimate (§6). Anything above the new ceilings also needs a code-ceiling PR. The owner's "next 10-12 bucks" is a direction, not such a word. Ruling 1121 §3.4: 0.50 is not worth it before 12-07.

No book has cleared the promotion gate. floor-lane-1 has not cleared the promotion gate. Nothing here says FLOOR makes money.

**Wallet-floor slots.** The manager computed them with the code's need formula, `tools/floor_arena_executor.py:184-193` at 5fc473b5. Each further open is checked at its own n: `need(n) = 400,000,000 + 160,000 + 2,100,000 + (n+1) x 1,600,000 + 750,000,000` (no unlanded held stakes assumed), refused when the balance is below it. Each open takes 402,260,000 from the balance (stake + send fee + rent).

- Immediately (6 open at 0.25):

#### Risk in plain words, recomputed at the ask (owner's words given below: no-top-up columns, and the 12-slot cap; the +1.0 column is WORKSHEET arithmetic inside that cap)

Convention as Am.14 and the worksheet: 0.40386 per 0.40 position, 0.25386 per 0.25 position. L = +1.065493048. W = 4.210914609 (free balance + open basis) before any top-up; a top-up is added to W in the +1.0 and +2.0 columns. Lifetime bound = 5.00 + L = **6.065493048 SOL**. "Remaining room" here means that lifetime bound; it is not reduced by the open worst case already used. One-day bound = min(room, 0.80 + stake at once).

| | No top-up, immediate | +1.0, immediate | +2.0, immediate | No top-up, steady | +1.0, steady | +2.0, steady |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| Slots stated (table above) | 4 | 7 | 8 | 8 | 11 | 12 |
| 0.25 positions open (k) | 6 | 6 | 6 | 0 | 0 | 0 |
| Most at stake at once = k x 0.25386 + slots x 0.40386 | 1.52316 + 1.61544 = **3.13860** | 1.52316 + 2.82702 = **4.35018** | 1.52316 + 3.23088 = **4.75404** | **3.23088** | **4.44246** | **4.84632** |
| One UTC day can lose = min(6.065493, 0.80 + stake) | **3.93860** | **5.15018** | **5.55404** | **4.03088** | **5.24246** | **5.64632** |
| Most the lane can still lose = 5.00 + L | 6.065493 | 6.065493 | 6.065493 | 6.065493 | 6.065493 | 6.065493 |
| Wallet at cost (W + top-up) | 4.210915 | 5.210915 | 6.210915 | 4.210915 | 5.210915 | 6.210915 |
| Lifetime bound / wallet at cost | 144.0% | 116.4% | 97.7% | 144.0% | 116.4% | 97.7% |
| (Wallet at cost - 0.75) / wallet at cost | 82.2% | 85.6% | 87.9% | 82.2% | 85.6% | 87.9% |

- From unrounded W and L: 6.065493048 / 4.210914609 = 1.4404 and (4.210914609 - 0.75) / 4.210914609 = 0.8219 with no top-up; 1.1640 and 0.8561 with +1.0; 0.9766 and 0.8792 with +2.0.

R2 one-day bound min(6.065493, 0.50 + 4.06176) = **4.56176**. S10 at most 16 x 0.10386 = **1.66176** at stake, one-day bound min(6.065493, 0.50 + 1.66176) = **2.16176**. The lifetime bound is **5.00 + realized = 6.065493048** at R1, R2 and S10.
- Evidence is thin, as Am.14 says: the LIVE-FIT edge is 4 days chosen after a good run, the November in-sample ex-top-3 is negative at every cap tested, and no live fill exists at 0.40.

**C3 disclosures (restated; Am.14 "Disclosures" controls).**
- (i) After any step-back the lifetime bound stays 5.00 + realized, not 4.00 + realized, until the budget is changed by its own T1 record. While 0.40 positions are open, new 0.25 entries are refused by `committed_loss_stop` or the full-wipe pre-send until enough of them close, for up to about 24 h. These are refusals, not a STOP.
- (ii) A step-back whose start line has any other tail, or whose loaded-config sha256 does not match, means the lane STOP file at once and one HELM-ASK to fix it.
- (iii) S10 is the one 0.10 target after the Am.14 install (Am.13 item 2 resumption after a Y STOP, and Am.13 exit 10 in a 0.25 population on R1/R2). It runs at budget 5.00, so its lifetime bound is 5.00 + realized.
